```diff
@@ -480,3 +549,175 @@ def test_search_state_round_trips(cls, family, out_dir):
     state = pickle.loads(pickle.dumps(gen.get_state()))
     gen.load_state(state)
     assert gen.generate_population(1) is not None
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# The nominal pose, plans/co_optimisation_audit_implementation.md phases 2 to 5
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+STATIC_ASSET = {
+    "biped": "environments/environments/assets/mjcf/solefoot/tron1/base_robot.xml",
+    "quadruped": "environments/environments/assets/mjcf/quadruped/quadruped.xml",
+}
+REPO = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+
+
+def _world(xml: str, qpos: dict | None = None) -> dict:
+    """World position of every body origin at the given hinge angles, root at the origin."""
+    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
+
+    def qmul(a, b):
+        w1, x1, y1, z1 = a
+        w2, x2, y2, z2 = b
+        return np.array([w1 * w2 - x1 * x2 - y1 * y2 - z1 * z2, w1 * x2 + x1 * w2 + y1 * z2 - z1 * y2,
+                         w1 * y2 - x1 * z2 + y1 * w2 + z1 * x2, w1 * z2 + x1 * y2 - y1 * x2 + z1 * w2])
+
+    def rot(q, v):
+        return qmul(qmul(q, np.array([0.0, *v])), q * np.array([1, -1, -1, -1]))[1:]
+
+    out = {}
+
+    def walk(el, p, q):
+        for b in el.findall("body"):
+            pos = np.array([float(v) for v in (b.get("pos") or "0 0 0").split()])
+            quat = np.array([float(v) for v in (b.get("quat") or "1 0 0 0").split()])
+            pw, qw = p + rot(q, pos), qmul(q, quat / np.linalg.norm(quat))
+            j = b.find("joint")
+            if j is not None and qpos and j.get("name") in qpos:
+                ax, a = np.array([float(v) for v in j.get("axis").split()]), qpos[j.get("name")]
+                qw = qmul(qw, np.array([np.cos(a / 2), *(np.sin(a / 2) * ax)]))
+            out[b.get("name")] = pw
+            walk(b, pw, qw)
+
+    walk(ET.fromstring(xml).find("worldbody"), np.zeros(3), np.array([1.0, 0, 0, 0]))
+    return out
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("family", FAMILIES)
+def test_design_spec_round_trips_through_a_dict(family, out_dir):
+    gen = U.RandomDesignGenerator(family=family, num_individuals=1, output_dir=out_dir)
+    cls = type(gen.baseline)
+    assert cls.from_dict(gen.baseline.to_dict()) == gen.baseline
+    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="unknown DesignSpec fields"):
+        cls.from_dict({**gen.baseline.to_dict(), "knee_fold": 1.0})
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("family", FAMILIES)
+def test_the_baseline_emits_the_committed_asset(family, out_dir):
+    gen = U.RandomDesignGenerator(family=family, num_individuals=1, output_dir=out_dir)
+    assert gen.family.build_xml(gen.baseline) == (REPO / STATIC_ASSET[family]).read_text()
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("family", FAMILIES)
+def test_the_isaaclab_document_moves_the_fold_into_the_frames(family, out_dir):
+    gen = U.RandomDesignGenerator(family=family, num_individuals=1, output_dir=out_dir)
+    b, root = gen.family.module.builder, gen.family.root_body
+    qpos = {}
+    if family == "quadruped":
+        for leg in b.LEG_NAMES:
+            qpos[f"{leg}_thigh_joint"] = b.THIGH_BEND_RAD
+            qpos[f"{leg}_calf_joint"] = b.SHANK_BEND_RAD
+    mujoco = _world(b.build_xml(gen.baseline), qpos)
+    isaac = _world(gen.family.build_xml(gen.baseline))
+    assert max(np.abs((mujoco[k] - mujoco[root]) - (isaac[k] - isaac[root])).max()
+               for k in isaac) < 1e-6
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("family", FAMILIES)
+def test_the_chain_is_the_emitted_document(family, out_dir):
+    gen = U.RandomDesignGenerator(family=family, num_individuals=8, output_dir=out_dir)
+    fam = gen.family
+    for idx in range(8):
+        spec, _ = gen._generate_individual(0, idx)
+        world = _world(fam.build_xml(spec))
+        rec = fam.nominal(spec)
+        foot, hip = fam.foot_bodies[0], fam.hip_bodies[0]
+        assert world[foot][0] - world[hip][0] == pytest.approx(rec["dx"], abs=2e-6)
+        assert -world[foot][2] == pytest.approx(rec["depth"], abs=2e-6)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("family", FAMILIES)
+def test_the_solved_hip_puts_the_foot_under_the_hip(family, out_dir):
+    gen = U.CMAESDesignGenerator(family=family, num_individuals=N, output_dir=out_dir)
+    rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
+    for _ in range(50):
+        parameters = {k: float(rng.uniform(lo, hi)) for k, (_, (lo, hi)) in gen.param_ranges.items()}
+        parameters["thigh_bend_offset"] = 0.0
+        spec = gen._spec_from_parameters(parameters)
+        # the hip's rounding to 3 dp remains
+        assert gen.family.nominal(spec)["dx"] == pytest.approx(gen._dx_target, abs=1e-3)
+        if gen.family.has_ankle:
+            total = spec.thigh_bend_offset + spec.knee_bend_offset + spec.ankle_bend_offset
+            assert total == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-12)
+        else:
+            assert not hasattr(spec, "ankle_bend_offset")
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("family", FAMILIES)
+def test_a_refused_pose_falls_back_to_the_last_feasible_design(family, out_dir):
+    gen = U.CMAESDesignGenerator(family=family, num_individuals=N, output_dir=out_dir)
+    good = gen._spec_from_parameters({"thigh_length_scale": 1.0, "shank_length_scale": 1.0,
+                                      "thigh_bend_offset": 0.05, "knee_bend_offset": 0.1})
+    lean = {"thigh_bend_offset": good.thigh_bend_offset + 0.6}
+    if gen.family.has_ankle:
+        lean["ankle_bend_offset"] = -(lean["thigh_bend_offset"] + good.knee_bend_offset)
+    bad = type(good).from_dict({**good.to_dict(), **lean})
+    # a 0.6 rad lean puts the foot far outside the tolerance the 0.10 rad draw sets
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="under the hip"):
+        U.check_nominal_pose(gen.family, bad, gen._dx_target, gen._lean_max())
+    assert gen._build_xml(bad) == gen.family.build_xml(gen.baseline)
+    assert gen._last_emitted is gen.baseline
+    gen._build_xml(good)
+    assert gen._build_xml(bad) == gen.family.build_xml(good)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("family", FAMILIES)
+def test_the_population_records_the_nominal_pose(family, out_dir):
+    gen = _make(U.GrowingDesignDistCMAESDesignGenerator, family, out_dir, late_start=True,
+                late_start_it=25, late_start_prior_pop_size=N)
+    pop = gen.generate_population(3)
+    nominal = pop.get_nominal()
+    assert nominal is not None and len(nominal) == N
+    lo, hi = gen.family.height_corridor
+    for spec, rec in zip(pop.get_specs(), nominal):
+        assert rec == gen.family.nominal(spec)
+        assert lo < rec["height"] < hi
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("family", FAMILIES)
+def test_joint_rot_is_the_documents_body_quat(family, out_dir):
+    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
+    gen = _make(U.CMAESDesignGenerator, family, out_dir, seed=5)
+    pop = gen.generate_population(0)
+    for spec, links in zip(pop.get_specs(), pop.get_link_length_params()):
+        root = ET.fromstring(gen.family.build_xml(spec)).find("worldbody")
+        by_joint = {}
+        for b in root.iter("body"):
+            j = b.find("joint")
+            by_joint[j.get("name") if j is not None else b.get("name")] = b
+        seen = 0
+        for entry in links.values():
+            for joint, quat in entry.get("joint_rot", {}).items():
+                doc = [float(v) for v in by_joint[joint].get("quat").split()]
+                assert np.allclose(doc, quat, atol=1e-6), joint
+                seen += 1
+        assert seen == (6 if family == "biped" else 8)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("family", FAMILIES)
+def test_zero_width_pose_ranges_search_the_lengths_alone(family, out_dir):
+    zero = {"thigh_bend_offset": ("offset", (0.0, 0.0)), "knee_bend_offset": ("offset", (0.0, 0.0))}
+    gen = _make(U.CMAESDesignGenerator, family, out_dir, param_ranges=zero)
+    for spec in gen.generate_population(0).get_specs():
+        assert spec.knee_bend_offset == 0.0
+        U.check_nominal_pose(gen.family, spec, gen._dx_target, gen._lean_max())
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("family", FAMILIES)
+def test_the_categorical_random_phase_draws_every_actuator(family, out_dir):
+    gen = _make(U.CatCMAESDesignGenerator, family, out_dir, late_start=True,
+                late_start_it=10, late_start_prior_pop_size=64)
+    specs = gen.generate_population(0).get_specs()
+    assert len(specs) == 64
+    for group in gen.cat_groups:
+        assert {getattr(s, gen.family.spec_fields[group]) for s in specs} == set(gen.cat_names)
```

### 5.4 Implementation instructions

1. Apply the docstring and import hunks, then the nominal pose block after `_quadruped_links`, then `Family` and `FAMILIES`, then the range block, removing the four old structures together.
2. Apply the `Population` hunks and the base class hunks in file order, including phase 1's `get_state` and `load_state`.
3. Apply the generator hunks, phase 2's categorical hunks with them.
4. `grep -n "_current_scales\|_sample_scales\|_spec_from_scales\|CMAES_PARAM_RANGES\|LENGTH_SCALE_FIELDS\|ACTUATOR_SCALE_KEYS\|replace(\|NOMINAL_HIP_REFERENCE" co_optimisation/co_optimisation/runners/usd_generator.py` must return nothing, and the same pattern over `co_optimisation scripts environments` must return nothing outside comments and the existing tests' own use of `dataclasses.replace`.
5. Apply the test changes and run the suite, section 9.3.

### 5.5 Verification and outcome

| check | result |
|---|---|
| chain against forward kinematics of the emitted document | 5.8e-7 and 1.1e-6 m in foot lead, 2.5e-7 and 3.6e-7 m in depth, 400 designs per robot, and `test_the_chain_is_the_emitted_document` |
| zero lean puts the foot on target | within the hip's rounding, `test_the_solved_hip_puts_the_foot_under_the_hip` |
| sole flat | the three offsets sum to zero within 1e-12 rad |
| a search of the lengths alone | `test_zero_width_pose_ranges_search_the_lengths_alone`, no design refused |
| a refused design falls back and the population follows it | `test_a_refused_pose_falls_back_to_the_last_feasible_design`, `test_the_population_records_the_nominal_pose` |
| CMA-ES and the growing prior sample four parameters | the four dimensional test, and the runs of section 9.4 printing `aCMA-ES ... in dimension 4` |
| typed ranges, the untyped form and the refusals | the range test |
| statistics over the box | the two tables of section 5.1, every design inside its corridor and its hardware range |

The outcome is a generator that emits, for any draw of the four searched parameters, a design whose foot is under its hip to within the drawn lean, whose sole is flat, whose height and joint nominals are inside their corridors, and whose nominal pose record travels with it into the population and the checkpoint.

| every solve and check in `usd_generator.py`, the design generator handed a finished spec (sections 4.5 and 11) | `solve_nominal_pose`, `check_nominal_pose`, `Family.chain` in `usd_generator.py`, the builders only emit | adhered |
| only the hip and knee offsets are drawn, the biped's ankle `-(hip + knee)` (section 4.4) | identical, the ankle computed from the rounded pair | adhered |
| `NOMINAL_HIP_REFERENCE`, `"solved"` recommended, `"default"` kept (section 4.4) | `"solved"` alone, the switch removed | divergence by the review's item 10 |
| `solve_nominal_pose` with a closed form for the quadruped and bisection on [-1, 1] for the biped (section 4.5) | bisection for both on the exact chain | divergence, the closed form is exact only at zero clearances, and one solver on one chain serves both robots |
| `DX_DONOR = {"quadruped": 0.0, "biped": 0.025204}` (section 4.5) | the target computed from the donor at construction, 0.0 and +0.013987 m | divergence, section 1 finding 2 |
| `check_nominal_pose`, foot under hip with tolerance `1.05 (l1 + l2) sin(lean)`, sole flat to 1e-9, corridors (0.18, 0.45) and (0.55, 1.05), hardware ranges narrowed by 0.9 (section 4.5) | identical, the lean widened by the hip's 5e-4 rad rounding | adhered, the allowance keeps a lengths only search from refusing every design |
| a refusal routed to `_build_xml`'s fallback with the reason logged (section 4.5) | identical, the population following the emitted spec | adhered, with finding 7 closed |

| ranges thigh (-0.10, 0.10), knee (-0.25, 0.25) rad (section 4.4) | identical | adhered |
| `CMAES_PARAM_KEYS` of four names replacing `CMAES_PARAM_RANGES`, `SPEC_PARAM_FIELDS` replacing `LENGTH_SCALE_FIELDS` (section 4.8) | identical | adhered |
| the six actuator scales deleted with `ACTUATOR_SCALE_KEYS`, the loop and the docstring lines, a caller passing one raises (section 4.8) | identical, with a named `DEPRECATED_PARAM_KEYS` for the message | adhered |

| `_nominal_offsets` the one place the pose is resolved, empty when no offset is drawn (section 4.8) | `solve_nominal_pose` called once from `_spec_from_parameters`, never empty | divergence, every specification carries a pose once the legacy document is gone |
| `:553` restricted by `CMAES_PARAM_KEYS`, `_denormalise` unpacks `(_, (lo, hi))`, two dimensions to four, `_check_dimension` refuses an old checkpoint (section 4.8) | identical | adhered |
| D1c, the constraint solved per design (section 1) | solved and checked per design | adhered |

## 6. Phase 5, carrying the pose into the simulation (D1b in place, D7, N1)

### 6.1 Investigation outcome

A design reaches a running scene by two routes. A respawn converts its document and spawns the USD, which already holds every frame the builder wrote, so it needs nothing beyond phases 3 and 4. The in place route edits the prototypes the last respawn established, and `update_articulation_links` writes the box scale and translate, the mass, inertia and centre of mass and the child joint's `physics:localPos0` (`update.py:143-165`) and no rotation, so a pose change would not reach the simulation, and it finds a box alone, so an actuator pick would not either (section 3.1). This phase adds the rotation and the housings, measures the pose every environment actually takes, and makes the spawn follow it.

Seven facts, each read in the source or measured, fix the design.

| # | fact | evidence |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `localRot0` is the body quat composed with the joint's `localRot1`, for fixed and revolute joints alike, so the in place write composes rather than overwrites | section 4.1, and 14 joints of two converted designs agreeing with the rule to 5.4e-7 (section 9.2) |
| 2 | `body_link_pose_w` runs a kinematic update on read and every root or joint state write invalidates it, so a probe needs no physics step | `articulation_data.py:583-598`, `articulation.py:439-443` and `:606-614` |
| 3 | `default_root_state` is rebuilt from the configuration whenever the articulation initialises, which every `sim.reset()` after a `sim.stop()` triggers, so the spawn height must be written again after every respawn and every in place update | `articulation.py:1666-1678`, `_process_cfg` called from `_initialize_impl` |
| 4 | the biped's sole depth below the foot body depends on the ankle actuator a categorical search picks, so the measurement compares the kinematic depth and the foot's lead over the hip, both independent of the sole, and the sole comes from the record | `biped/builder.py:380-381`, section 1 finding 3 |
| 5 | the in place edit written through environment 0 reaches environment 1, which shares the design and was never written, for geometry, `localPos0` and `localRot0` alike | section 9.2, design B measured in both environments |
| 6 | a housing cylinder's size is the `radius` and `height` of the cylinder prim under a unit scale, authored in the design's base layer, and the importer writes its `extent` about x for a cylinder whose `axis` is z, so the editor reproduces that extent to leave a written design identical to a converted one | `MjcfUsd.cpp:799-818`, and a converted biped dumped prim by prim, `abad_R_act/collisions/_geom_3/_geom_3` holding `radius` 0.0483, `height` 0.0545 and `extent` `(+-0.02725, +-0.0483, +-0.0483)` |
| 7 | PhysX re-cooks a collider from the stage at the `sim.reset()` that follows the edit, and takes masses and inertias from the body prims there too, so the edit needs no tensor write | the cooked collider volume read through `omni.physx`'s property query and the masses and inertias of the articulation view, written in place against converted, section 9.2 |

N1 had two halves. The propagation of physics attributes is now measured (fact 5) and is checked in every run by extending `assert_link_lengths_applied` to the environment `d + num_individuals` of each design. The ordering of environment prims is settled by `find_matching_prims`, which walks `GetAllChildren` in authored order (`isaaclab/sim/utils/queries.py`), the order the cloner creates `env_0` to `env_N-1`, and is asserted at every respawn rather than printed once, the comparison costing one traversal of the environment scope.

Every specification now carries a pose (section 5.1), so every population carries a nominal record and every link payload carries the body frames, and neither the payload builders nor the runner has a branch for a population without them, the review's item 11. A copt checkpoint written before this plan has no record, and it is refused before its population is read, by the estimator's width and by `_check_dimension` (section 10.2).

### 6.2 Architecture and rationale

```
Family.links(spec)              thigh entry: joint_rot {thigh fixed joint: q_thigh, knee hinge: q_shank}
(phase 4 record)                biped shank entry: joint_rot {ankle_{S}_act fixed joint: q_ankle}
                                housing entries: radius, half_length, mass, inertia,
                                  the biped's parent_body, parent_joint, parent_pos, its thigh's too
                                biped foot entry: the sole's box, mass, inertia
        │
        ▼
update_articulation_links       localRot0 := q_body * localRot1 for every named joint, on its source layer
        │                        a cylinder's radius, height and extent on its prototype's cylinder prim
        │                        localPos0 of the child joint and of the body's own joint
        │                        box, mass, inertia, centre of mass as before
        ▼
sim.reset()                     articulation re-initialised, default_root_state back to the configuration
        ▼
apply_standing_height           1. pitch nominals must be zero
(update.py)                     2. every robot at its nominal joints, upright, root at z = 2.0
                                3. foot depth and foot lead over the hip axis against Family.nominal, 2 mm
                                4. default_root_state[:, 2] = depth + sole + 0.028
        ▼
env.copt_nominal_base_height    (num_envs,), the measured standing height, read by phase 6's terms
```

| call site | sequence after this phase | reset by `_apply_nominal_pose` |
|---|---|---|
| bootstrap, `_reload_morphology` (`:576`) | respawn, which resets, then measure, then reset again so the robots stand at their own height, then actuators | yes |
| late start, `_update_morphology` respawning (`:698`) | the same | yes |
| in place, `_update_morphology` (`:701-723`) | `apply_link_length_params`, measure, the existing reset, the propagation check, actuators | no, the existing reset follows |
| resume, `_apply_restored_state` (`:609`) | `apply_link_length_params`, measure, `restore_env_state`, whose `reset_to` places the robots | no |

| decision | alternative rejected | reason |
|---|---|---|
| compose with the joint's own `localRot1` | write the body quat bare (audit section 4.9) | fact 1, the bare write turns every knee about local x |
| measure depth and lead, add the sole from the record | measure the sole and use a family constant (audit section 4.10) | fact 4 |
| a 2 mm tolerance on both | depth alone | the lean changes the height only to second order, so the lead is the measurement that sees a missing hip rotation |
| raise on disagreement | log and continue | a design whose measured pose is not its record would train against a reward target for another design |
| keep the zero nominal assertion although every nominal is now zero | trust the configuration | one comparison guards against a configuration that reintroduces a fold in the nominals, the double fold the review's item 7 removes |
| write a cylinder's `radius`, `height` and `extent` on the cylinder prim | scale the cylinder's `Xform` as a box is scaled | the importer leaves that `Xform` at a unit scale (fact 6), and a scaled unit cylinder would not be the prim a respawn creates, so the in place design would differ from its conversion |
| a housing placed by `parent_body`, `parent_joint` and `parent_pos` on its own entry | a child placement on the parent's entry, as the links are placed | the trunk carries both abduction housings, an entry naming one child, and the housing's own entry keeps its size and its placement, which the same pick sets, together |
| the propagation check reads every placement and every mass the payload names | the child separations alone | the categorical edit moves housings and masses, which the separations of the links do not see |
| one payload for both robots, the quadruped's housings carrying their fixed sizes | a quadruped payload of masses alone | the editor's path is the same for both robots, and the rewritten size equals the document's |

D7 closes with this phase. Every environment spawns 28 mm above its own design's standing height, the margin the quadruped asset documents (`quadruped_identified_cfg.py:89-91`), and `reset_root_state_uniform` reads it at every reset. The joint reset noise of up to 0.2 rad that the audit's D7 table notes is unchanged, and the margin was measured against no noise, as it was before.

### 6.3 Edits

The link payloads, `co_optimisation/runners/usd_generator.py:79-151`. `_entry`'s child becomes optional, for the biped's sole, and `_housing` gives a housing's entry from its builder's own `cyl_inertia`. The biped's thigh entry takes the box and child positions of the rotated thigh frame and the placement `_cluster_layout` gives it, both families attach `joint_rot` unconditionally, and the biped adds its four housings, each placed from `_cluster_layout` but the ankle housing, which the shank's child placement already carries, and its sole. The quadruped's `joint_rot` and housing block is the first part of the phase 4 hunk that follows `_quadruped_links`.

```diff
+def _housing(builder, actuator_mass, radius, half_length, parent=None) -> dict:
+    """One actuator housing, a cylinder whose size and mass follow the actuator the design picks.
+
+    ``radius`` and ``half_length`` are the MuJoCo cylinder size, which the importer
+    authors as the radius and twice the height of a ``UsdGeomCylinder``
+    (MjcfUsd.cpp:799-818). The inertia is the builder's own, about the cylinder's
+    principal axes, which the document fixes. ``parent`` is ``(body, joint, pos)``,
+    the housing's own origin in its parent body, which that joint's
+    ``physics:localPos0`` holds.
+    """
+    i_perp, i_axial = builder.cyl_inertia(float(actuator_mass), radius, 2.0 * half_length)
+    out = {

+        "half_length": float(half_length),
+        "mass": float(actuator_mass),
+        "inertia": [i_perp, i_perp, i_axial],

+    if parent is not None:

+    return out
+
+
```

The rotation write, the cylinder, the parent placement, the propagation and mass check and `apply_standing_height`, `co_optimisation/utils/update.py:49`, `:80`, `:91`, `:108-165`, `:223`, `:243-284`, the last function appended at the end of the module.

```diff
+def _cylinder(prototype) -> Usd.Prim | None:
+    """The one ``UsdGeomCylinder`` under *prototype*.
+
+    An actuator housing carries one visual and one collision cylinder, in different
+    prototypes. Unlike the box, whose size is the scale of the referencing ``Xform``,
+    the importer authors a cylinder's size as the ``radius`` and ``height`` of the
+    cylinder prim itself under a unit scale (MjcfUsd.cpp:799-818,
+    MjcfImporter.cpp:428-444).
+    """
+    for prim in Usd.PrimRange(prototype):
+        if prim.IsA(UsdGeom.Cylinder):
+            return prim
+    return None
+

+
+def apply_standing_height(
+    env: ManagerBasedRLEnv,
+    nominal: list[dict],
+    foot_bodies: tuple[str, ...],
+    hip_bodies: tuple[str, ...],
+    pitch_joints: list[str],
+    spawn_margin: float = 0.028,
+    tolerance: float = 0.002,
+):
+    """Measure each environment's nominal pose, assert it against its design's, set the spawn.
+
+    The foot depth and the foot's lead over the hip pitch axis are compared with
+    ``Family.nominal``, computed from the chain the document was built from, so
+    one comparison covers the solve, the builder's frames, the importer, the in
+    place editor and the propagation to every environment. Each environment then
+    spawns *spawn_margin* above its design's standing height, which
+    ``reset_root_state_uniform`` reads at every reset. Runs after the
+    ``sim.reset()`` that re-initialises the articulation and before the
+    ``env.reset()`` that places the robots, and takes no physics step,
+    ``body_link_pose_w`` updating the kinematics on read
+    (articulation_data.py:583-598).
+
+    Returns:
+        The standing height (num_envs,).
+    """
+    import torch
+
+    robot = env.scene.articulations["robot"]
+    device = robot.device
+    assignment = torch.arange(env.num_envs, device=device) % len(nominal)
+
+    # The frames carry the fold, so a nonzero pitch nominal would apply it twice.
+    ids, _ = robot.find_joints(pitch_joints)
+    stale = robot.data.default_joint_pos[:, ids].abs().max().item()
+    if stale > 1e-6:
+        raise RuntimeError(
+            f"pitch joint nominals of up to {stale:.4f} rad in the asset configuration, "
+            "while the document folds the leg in its body frames. Set them to zero."
+        )
+
+    robot.write_joint_state_to_sim(robot.data.default_joint_pos,
+                                   torch.zeros_like(robot.data.default_joint_vel))
+    probe = robot.data.default_root_state[:, :7].clone()
+    probe[:, :3] = env.scene.env_origins
+    probe[:, 2] = 2.0
+    probe[:, 3:7] = torch.tensor([1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0], device=device)
+    robot.write_root_pose_to_sim(probe)
+    foot_ids, _ = robot.find_bodies(list(foot_bodies), preserve_order=True)
+    hip_ids, _ = robot.find_bodies(list(hip_bodies), preserve_order=True)
+    pos = robot.data.body_link_pos_w
+    depth = probe[:, 2:3] - pos[:, foot_ids, 2]                         # (num_envs, legs)
+    lead = pos[:, foot_ids, 0] - pos[:, hip_ids, 0]
+
+    record = lambda key: torch.tensor([n[key] for n in nominal], device=device)[assignment]
+    worst_depth = (depth - record("depth").unsqueeze(1)).abs().max().item()
+    worst_dx = (lead - record("dx").unsqueeze(1)).abs().max().item()
+    if worst_depth > tolerance or worst_dx > tolerance:
+        raise RuntimeError(
+            f"measured nominal pose differs from the solved one by {worst_depth * 1e3:.2f} mm "
+            f"in foot depth and {worst_dx * 1e3:.2f} mm in foot lead over the hip"
+        )
+
+    # The margin keeps a rough terrain tile from capturing a foot at reset.
+    standing = depth.mean(dim=1) + record("sole")
+    robot.data.default_root_state[:, 2] = standing + spawn_margin
+    print(
+        f"[copt] nominal pose measured for {env.num_envs} environments, standing height "
+        f"[{standing.min().item():.4f}, {standing.max().item():.4f}] m, worst disagreement "
+        f"{1e3 * max(worst_depth, worst_dx):.3f} mm"
+    )
+    return standing
```

The runner, `co_optimisation/runners/copt_on_policy_runner.py`. The import of `apply_standing_height` and `isaaclab.sim`, the nominal record in the checkpoint's population, `_respawn_population`'s ordering assertion, and `_apply_nominal_pose`. The `_suppress_curriculum` lines are D6's and are discussed in phase 7.


### 6.4 Implementation instructions

1. Apply the three link payload hunks of `usd_generator.py` together with phase 4's, the file being edited once.
2. Apply the six `update.py` hunks.
3. Apply the runner hunks, including phase 7's constructor hunk, then confirm `grep -n "_apply_nominal_pose" co_optimisation/co_optimisation/runners/copt_on_policy_runner.py` lists the definition and four calls.
4. The `joint_rot` test of phase 4 checks the payload against the document. The simulator check is the validation run of section 9.8, whose log must print `nominal pose measured` at every swap with a worst disagreement under 2 mm.

### 6.5 Verification and outcome

| check | result |
|---|---|
| rotation rule against the importer's own output | 14 pose joints of two designs, section 9.2 |
| a design spawned from its USD stands at its record | section 9.2, both robots |
| a design written in place stands at its record, in the written and the unwritten environment | section 9.2, both robots |
| a design written in place, its actuators changed, is its conversion | every body pose, mass, inertia, centre of mass and cooked collider volume within 7.2e-7, section 9.2, both robots |
| every swap of a training run | every environment measured within 0.006 mm of its record at every bootstrap, in place and late start swap, the categorical search's in place swaps among them, and after a resume, section 9.4 |
| the mirror row propagation check | passed at every in place swap of the training runs |
| the ordering assertion | passed at every respawn of the training runs |
| a resumed run restores the record | each resumed run measured the checkpointed population's heights exactly as saved, section 9.4 |

The outcome is a pose that every environment demonstrably stands at, by either route, with the spawn following it, and a run that stops at the first swap where any of that fails rather than training on a design it does not have.

### 6.6 Review against the audit

| audit statement | plan | verdict |
|---|---|---|
| the respawn route carries the pose via USD, the in place route needs `physics:localRot0` (section 4.9) | identical | adhered |
| `joint_rot` in the entries `_quadruped_links` and `_biped_links` return, built from the builder's `pose_frames` (section 4.9) | identical, for every design | adhered |
| the biped's `_biped_links` box and child in the frame scheme, `pos=(0, 0, -l1/2)`, `child_pos=(0, 0, -l1)` (section 4.9) | identical, for every design | adhered |
| `localRot0 = Gf.Quatf(*quat)`, the body quat written bare (section 4.9) | `Gf.Quatf(*quat) * localRot1` | divergence, section 1 finding 1, measured |
| the expected donor quats of section 4.9 to be compared at validation | the thigh and knee body quats match, `localRot0` of a hinge is not the body quat | divergence, the expectation is restated as the composition, which the container check tests |
| `apply_link_length_params` needs no signature change (section 4.9) | none | adhered |
| `apply_standing_height` asserts zero pitch nominals, probes at z = 2.0, asserts against the solved height to 2 mm, writes `default_root_state[:, 2]`, margin 0.028 (section 4.10) | identical, asserting depth and lead and adding the recorded sole | adhered, the measured quantity changed by fact 4 |
| `Family` gains `standing_height`, `foot_bodies`, `foot_sole_offset`, `pitch_joints` (section 4.10) | `chain`, `foot_bodies`, `hip_bodies`, `pitch_joints` and `Family.nominal(spec)`, the height and sole per design | divergence, a family constant cannot carry a sole that follows the ankle pick |
| `Population` gains the heights and the nominal pose with `None` defaults (section 4.10) | one `nominal` record list, `None` by default | adhered |
| call sites `_update_morphology`, `_respawn_population`, `_apply_restored_state`, publishing `copt_nominal_base_height` and `copt_nominal_pose` (section 4.10) | `_reload_morphology`, both branches of `_update_morphology` and `_apply_restored_state`, through `_apply_nominal_pose`, `_respawn_population` itself left as the shared respawn, publishing `copt_nominal_base_height` alone | adhered, the respawn's two callers measure rather than the helper, so the resume path, which respawns and then edits in place, measures once, and divergence in `copt_nominal_pose`, which nothing reads once the observation reads the joint frames, section 7 |
| N1, extend `assert_link_lengths_applied` to environment `d + num_individuals` (section 6) | identical, and the check reads every placement and mass the payload names | adhered, extended to the housings |
| the categorical search's swap route, which the audit's findings do not address, its section 10 recording this plan's own finding | in place, the housings and the sole in the payload and the cylinder in the editor | addition, the second review's item 2 |
| N1, print the first twenty matched prim paths at the first respawn (section 6) | an assertion that the matched order is `scene.env_prim_paths`, at every respawn | adhered, strengthened from a print to an assertion |
| D7, closed by step 4 of `apply_standing_height` (section 5) | identical | adhered |
| the action offset staleness hazard closes, `default_joint_pos` zero for every design (section 4.7) | the zero nominals of section 4 and the assertion of step 1 | adhered |

## 7. Phase 6, every term that depends on the nominal pose (D1, section 4.11 of the audit)

### 7.1 Investigation outcome

The requirement is a v2 for each reward and termination term that takes the updated pose into account. The audit's reading, kept here, is that a v2 is written for each term whose value or configured parameter depends on the pose, and that a term which provably does not is listed with the reason, its invariance standing in for a copy that would change nothing. Every v2 below reproduces its original exactly while the runner has published no standing height, which is the case before the first swap and in every play configuration, and at the donor's height in every environment.

The callers of each original, enumerated per the workspace's rule 3, confirm the audit's lists with two corrections.

| original | callers | correction to the audit's section 4.11 |
|---|---|---|
| `base_height_rough_l2` (`rewards.py:1975`) | `quadruped/base_env_cfg.py:1086`, `SF/kscale_base_env_cfg.py:888`, `SF/limx_base_env_cfg.py:937`, `SF/brs_base_env_cfg.py:749` | none |
| `feet_regulation` (`rewards.py:1936`) | `SF/limx_base_env_cfg.py:974`, `SF/brs_base_env_cfg.py:786`, `SF/kscale_base_env_cfg.py:1016`, `PF/limx_base_env_cfg.py:398` | none |
| `feet_air_time` (`rewards.py:338`) | `SF/limx_base_env_cfg.py:1009`, `SF/limx_berkeley_env_cfg.py:306` | two callers, not four, the KScale (`:1039`) and SD_BRS1 (`:799`) configurations calling `feet_air_time_positive_biped` |
| `feet_air_time_v2` (`rewards.py:368`) | `quadruped/base_env_cfg.py:1132` | none |
| `foot_clearance_reward_v4` (`rewards.py:752`) | `quadruped/base_env_cfg.py:1163` | the v2's name `foot_clearance_reward_v5` is taken at `rewards.py:856`, so it is `foot_clearance_reward_v6` |

Every existing function accepts a per environment tensor where it takes a scalar parameter, except `base_height_rough_l2`, whose `torch.nan_to_num` takes a float. So four of the five v2 functions read the published height, form a `(num_envs, 1)` tensor and call the original with it, and only the base height v2 carries a body of its own. A v2 that delegates cannot drift from its original.

Both copt tasks share their `RewardsCfg` with plain tasks (section 1, finding 4), and the second review asks that each robot's tasks stay in that robot's file, shared where they agree and given a class of their own where they differ. Each file therefore gains `CoptRewardsCfg(RewardsCfg)`, which redefines the terms whose function or parameters change and inherits every other term, and the copt environment names it where it named `RewardsCfg`. A configclass field redefined in a subclass keeps its parent's position, so the reward manager meets the terms in the order it met them before, and the dumped configuration of every copt task is the one the second draft's `__post_init__` produced, term for term (section 9.6). The plain and HIM tasks keep `RewardsCfg` and are unchanged. The curricula follow the same pattern in phase 7.

The estimator target needs the nominal pose itself, and the second review's item 3 asks that it be read from what the document folded into the articulation rather than handed over by the runner. With the fold in the body frames every design's `default_joint_pos` is zero, and Isaac Lab exposes no joint frame, neither in `ArticulationData` nor in the PhysX articulation view (section 1, finding 19). The fold lives on the stage, where the importer wrote each folded body's quat into its incoming joint as `localRot0 = q_body * localRot1` (section 4.1, finding 1), so `localRot0 * localRot1^-1` is the body quat and twice the arctangent of its y and w components the fold about the pitch axis. `robot_nominal_pose` becomes a class term, as `robot_link_lengths` is, that finds each named body's incoming joint once on environment 0, reads that joint's two frames in every environment, and keeps the result until the articulation's PhysX view changes, which a `sim.reset()` causes, and in a run only a respawn, an in place write and a resume call one. A term that read the link kinematics instead would recover the fold of a welded body exactly but the knee's only with the sign of its axis, which is a stage fact, so the stage is the one source for both. The read costs 0.40 to 0.65 s per 1024 environments (section 9.2), 2.4 to 3.9 s at 6144 environments for each of the two terms, the morphology group's and the critic's, once a swap, every 480 iterations, and the term refuses a body folded about any axis but y rather than report a wrong angle.

The air time thresholds scale with the square root of the standing height. A pendulum of length h swings with a period proportional to sqrt(h / g), so a stride timing tuned on a robot of height h0 becomes, on a robot of height h, the same timing multiplied by sqrt(h / h0). This is a modelling assumption of this plan, carried over from the audit, and the fallback keeps the donor's timing exact.

### 7.2 Architecture and rationale

```
runner, after every swap   env.copt_nominal_base_height  (num_envs,)
                                      │
            ┌─────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────┐
            ▼                         ▼                           ▼
base_height_rough_l2_v2    feet_regulation_v2          feet_air_time_v3 (quadruped)
 target = h per env         gate scale = h per env      feet_air_time_nominal (biped)
 fallback target_height     fallback base_height_target  thresholds x sqrt(h / h_ref)
                                                         fallback x 1
                           foot_clearance_reward_v6
                            target = frac x h per env
                            fallback target_height

stage, the joint frames     localRot0 * localRot1^-1 of each folded body's joint
                                      │   re-read when asset.root_physx_view changes
                                      ▼
                           robot_nominal_pose, (num_envs, folded bodies), radians about +y
                            morphologyObs and critic of both copt tasks
```

| term | robot | depends on the pose through | v2 | change, set in the copt task alone |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `pen_base_height` | both | the target, 0.292 and 0.75, against standing heights of 0.19 to 0.41 m and 0.64 to 0.98 m | `base_height_rough_l2_v2` | per environment target, fallback 0.292 and 0.804 |
| `pen_feet_regulation` | biped | the gate's length scale `base_height_target`, and `foot_radius` 0.03 against a sole 0.050 below the foot body | `feet_regulation_v2` | gate scale per environment, fallback and `base_height_target` 0.804, `foot_radius` 0.050 |
| `rew_foot_clearance` | quadruped | `target_height` 0.10, 34 per cent of the donor's height | `foot_clearance_reward_v6` | `target_height_frac = 0.10 / 0.292` of each environment's height, fallback 0.10 |
| `feet_air_time` | quadruped | thresholds 0.15 and 0.45 s tuned at 0.292 m | `feet_air_time_v3` over `feet_air_time_v2` | thresholds x sqrt(h / 0.292) |
| `feet_air_time` | biped | thresholds 0.2 and 0.5 s tuned at 0.804 m | `feet_air_time_nominal` over `feet_air_time` | thresholds x sqrt(h / 0.804) |
| estimator target and critic | both | the fold is a design variable now | `robot_nominal_pose`, a new observation read from the joint frames | the thigh and shank of every leg, 8 channels, for the quadruped, and the thigh, shank and ankle housing, 6 channels, for the biped, in `morphologyObs` and in the critic beside `link_lengths` |

The terms that stay, each with the reason it is invariant, as the audit's section 4.11 lists them and as this plan re-read them in the two configurations. With every nominal zero, a term that measures a joint against its nominal measures it against the design's own stance (section 4.1).

| term | robot | why it does not depend on the pose |
|---|---|---|
| `pen_foot_landing_vel` | quadruped | `foot_radius` is the sphere, fixed, and `about_landing_threshold` is a clearance above the terrain |
| `pen_feet_distance` | biped | a lateral separation, set by the trunk and the abduction nominal, while every offset is sagittal |
| `rew_keep_ankle_pitch_zero_in_air` | biped | targets joint angle zero, which is the flat sole because the fold lives in the frames |
| `pen_joint_pos_limits` | both | reads the soft limits, which follow the document's ranges at every initialisation |
| `pen_abad_deviation` | quadruped | the abduction nominal, held at zero |
| `pen_flat_orientation`, `pen_lin_vel_z`, `pen_ang_vel_xy`, `rew_lin_vel_xy`, `rew_ang_vel_z`, `keep_balance`, `rew_no_fly` | both | read the base velocity, the gravity projection or the contact state |
| `pen_joint_torque`, `pen_joint_accel`, `pen_joint_vel_l2`, `pen_action_rate`, `pen_action_smoothness` | both | no pose input, the torque magnitude following the lengths and masses as before |
| `pen_feet_impact`, `pen_undesired_contacts`, `feet_slide`, `pen_feet_hold` | both | thresholds on contact force and slip, which the masses move and the pose does not |
| termination `time_out` | both | none |
| termination `base_contact` | both | a contact force on the trunk, invariant as a function, its guard being the corridor of phase 4 and the spawn of phase 5 |

```diff
+    height = asset.data.root_pos_w[:, 2].unsqueeze(1) - sensor.data.ray_hits_w[:, :, 2]
+    fill = target.unsqueeze(1).expand_as(height)
+    height = torch.where(torch.isfinite(height), height, fill)
+    return torch.square(height.mean(dim=1) - target)
```

The nominal pose observation, `environments/.../mdp/observations.py`, its imports and the class appended. The two other hunks of this file are D2's and appear in phase 7.

```diff
@@ -1,5 +1,6 @@
 from __future__ import annotations
 
+import math
 import torch
 from collections.abc import Sequence
 from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
@@ -17,6 +18,8 @@ from isaaclab.sensors import (
     RayCasterCamera,
     TiledCamera,
 )
+from isaaclab.sim.utils.stage import get_current_stage
+from pxr import Usd, UsdPhysics
 
 if TYPE_CHECKING:
     from isaaclab.envs import ManagerBasedEnv, ManagerBasedRLEnv
@@ -267,3 +271,57 @@ class robot_link_lengths(ManagerTermBase):
             self._cache = delta.norm(dim=-1).clone()
             torch.set_printoptions(precision=10)
         return self._cache
+
+
+class robot_nominal_pose(ManagerTermBase):
+    """The fold of each named body about +y against its parent at zero joint position, (num_envs, num_bodies).
+
+    The co-optimisation documents fold the leg in the body frames (``pose_frames`` of
+    both design_generator builders), so ``default_joint_pos`` is zero for every design
+    and the nominal pose lives in the joint frames alone. Neither ``ArticulationData``
+    nor the PhysX articulation view exposes a joint frame, so the fold is read on the
+    stage from each body's incoming joint as ``localRot0 * localRot1^-1``, the
+    importer authoring ``localRot0`` as the body quat composed with ``localRot1``
+    (MjcfImporter.cpp:1341-1366). The read runs whenever the articulation has been
+    re-initialised, which a respawn and an in place swap both do, since either may
+    change the frames.
+    """
+
+    def __init__(self, cfg: ObservationTermCfg, env: ManagerBasedEnv):
+        super().__init__(cfg, env)
+        self._view = None
+        self._fold: torch.Tensor | None = None
+
+    def __call__(self, env: ManagerBasedEnv, asset_cfg: SceneEntityCfg) -> torch.Tensor:
+        asset: Articulation = env.scene[asset_cfg.name]
+        if self._view is not asset.root_physx_view:
+            self._fold = self._read(env, asset, asset_cfg)
+            self._view = asset.root_physx_view
+        return self._fold
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _read(env: ManagerBasedEnv, asset: Articulation, asset_cfg: SceneEntityCfg) -> torch.Tensor:
+        stage = get_current_stage()
+        ids = asset_cfg.body_ids
+        bodies = asset.body_names[ids] if isinstance(ids, slice) else [asset.body_names[i] for i in ids]
+        rel = asset.cfg.prim_path[len(env.scene.env_regex_ns):]
+        root = stage.GetPrimAtPath(env.scene.env_prim_paths[0] + rel)
+        joints = {}
+        for prim in Usd.PrimRange(root):
+            if prim.IsA(UsdPhysics.Joint):
+                targets = UsdPhysics.Joint(prim).GetBody1Rel().GetTargets()
+                if targets and targets[0].name in bodies:
+                    joints[targets[0].name] = prim.GetPath().MakeRelativePath(root.GetPath()).pathString
+        missing = [b for b in bodies if b not in joints]
+        if missing:
+            raise ValueError(f"no joint under {root.GetPath()} has {missing} as its child body")
+        fold = torch.empty(env.num_envs, len(bodies), dtype=torch.float64)
+        for i, env_path in enumerate(env.scene.env_prim_paths):
+            for k, body in enumerate(bodies):
+                joint = stage.GetPrimAtPath(f"{env_path}{rel}/{joints[body]}")
+                q = joint.GetAttribute("physics:localRot0").Get() * joint.GetAttribute("physics:localRot1").Get().GetInverse()
+                x, y, z = q.GetImaginary()
+                if max(abs(x), abs(z)) > 1e-4:
+                    raise ValueError(f"{joint.GetPath()} folds about an axis other than y, {q}")
+                fold[i, k] = math.remainder(2.0 * math.atan2(y, q.GetReal()), 2.0 * math.pi)
+        return fold.to(device=env.device, dtype=torch.float32)
```

### 7.4 Implementation instructions

1. Append the five reward functions and the observation class, with the observation module's two imports. Both modules are star exported by `mdp/__init__.py`, after `isaaclab_tasks`' own velocity `mdp`, and none of the six names exists in either, so no import changes.
2. Apply the two configuration files' hunks. Each adds classes beside the shared ones and changes only the copt environment's fields, so the plain and HIM tasks of both robots resolve to the configuration they resolve to today, the biped's critic gaining one field that defaults to None (section 10.2).
3. Run the invariance check of appendix B in the training container.

### 7.5 Verification and outcome

| check | result |
|---|---|
| each v2 with the attribute absent equals its original | exactly, maximum difference 0.0, section 9.5 |
| each v2 at the donor's height in every environment equals its original | exactly, except the quadruped air time at one single precision step |
| each v2 at per environment heights equals its original evaluated per environment at that environment's scaled parameter | exactly for the base height, the regulation and the clearance, to one single precision step for both air time terms |
| the observation against the generator's fold | within 1.1e-6 rad of `pose_frames` for both designs of both robots, a converted document's quaternions being written to six decimals (`_quat_y`), section 9.2 |
| the observation after an in place write | re-read through the new PhysX view, within 1.0e-6 rad of the written design, and within 1.2e-7 rad of the fold the bodies realise at zero joint angles, section 9.2 |
| the reward classes against the second draft's wiring | every copt task's rewards resolve to the same terms, functions, parameters and weights, section 9.6 |
| the copt tasks load and train with the classes | every run of section 9.4, the quadruped's estimator output 216 wide (8 + 20 + 180 + 8) and the biped's 160 (154 + 6), and the critics 80 and 60 wider for the ten steps of the fold |

The outcome is a reward set in which no term charges a design for standing at the height and fold the search gave it, held in each robot's own copt reward class, and an estimator and a critic given the fold the articulation actually carries.

### 7.6 Review against the audit

| audit statement | plan | verdict |
|---|---|---|
| a v2 for each term that depends on the pose, the invariant terms listed with their reason (section 4.11) | five v2 functions, the invariant table reproduced | adhered |
| callers enumerated per rule 3 (section 4.11) | re-enumerated | adhered, `feet_air_time`'s caller count corrected from four to two |
| `base_height_rough_l2_v2` with a per environment target and a scalar fallback, missed rays filled with the target (section 4.11) | identical, delegating to the original while unpublished | adhered |
| `feet_regulation_v2`, the gate scale per environment, `foot_radius` 0.050 by configuration (section 4.11) | identical | adhered |
| `foot_clearance_reward_v5` with `target_height_frac` 0.3425 (section 4.11) | `foot_clearance_reward_v6`, `target_height_frac = 0.10 / 0.292` | divergence in name only, `v5` exists at `rewards.py:856`, the fraction written as its own derivation |
| `feet_air_time_v3` over `feet_air_time_v2`, thresholds x sqrt(h / 0.292) (section 4.11) | identical | adhered |
| `feet_air_time_nominal` over `feet_air_time`, thresholds x sqrt(h / 0.804) (section 4.11) | identical | adhered |
| the wiring in "the copt RewardsCfg only" (section 4.11) | `CoptRewardsCfg(RewardsCfg)` in each robot's file, named by its copt environment alone | adhered, the class the audit names created by the second review's item 1 |
| the biped's fallback target from 0.75 to 0.804 in the copt task (section 4.11) | identical | adhered |
| `robot_morphology_v2`, the offsets and the standing height concatenated to the lengths (section 4.11) | `robot_nominal_pose`, the fold of every folded body read from its joint frame, a new term in the same `morphologyObs` group and in the critic, the group's concatenation doing the joining | adhered in substance, the fold replacing the offsets and the height, which it and the lengths determine, and `robot_link_lengths` left untouched rather than versioned |
| invariance tests at the donor, zero offsets reproducing the old reward to float precision (section 4.13 step 9) | appendix B, section 9.5 | adhered |

## 8. Phase 7, the divergence surfaces (D2, D5, D6)

### 8.1 Investigation outcome

D2 has three parts, the architecture, the loss weight and the target, and all three stand as the audit records them, with one adjustment each. The architecture change is a configuration edit to `PFQuadrupedCoptPPORunnerCfg` alone (`quadruped_rsl_rl_ppo_cfg.py:55-63`). The loss coefficient cannot ride on `RslRlPpoAlgorithmMlpCfg`, which the plain tasks hand to `PPO`, so it is a `CoptPPO` keyword set in `train.py`'s copt branch (section 1, finding 9). The target composition the audit wrote adds `robot_joint_pos`, which reads `default_joint_pos`, zero for every design after phase 3, so it is replaced by phase 6's `robot_nominal_pose` (finding 11), and the audit's restriction to the thigh and shank links is widened to the twelve housings, whose masses a categorical search moves (finding 20). The widths the change produces were read from the networks the runs of section 9.4 built.

| | quadruped before | quadruped after | biped before | biped after |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `policy` | `["policy", "morphologyObs"]` | `["policy"]` | `["policy"]` | unchanged |
| `encoderIn` | `["morphologyObs", "historyObs"]` | `["historyObs"]` | `["historyObs"]` | unchanged |
| `gtEncoderOut` | `["privilegedDynamicsObs"]` | `["morphologyObs"]` | `["morphologyObs"]` | unchanged |
| `morphologyObs` width | 258 | 216, 8 lengths, 20 masses, 180 inertias, 8 folds | 154 | 160, the 6 folds added |
| actor input width, the policy group and the estimator's output | 354 | 264 | 190 | 196 |
| critic input width, ten steps of the critic group | 80 fewer | 6690 | 60 fewer | 4890 |
| estimator input width | 6133 | 5875 | 5575 | 5575 |
| estimator output width | 48 | 216 | 154 | 160 |

D5 stands. The flag's record must be a file the run writes, and `params/agent.yaml` serialises the configclass rather than the dict the runner receives (finding 8), so `train.py` writes that dict as `params/copt_agent.yaml`. The write-through of the learning rate to the optimiser's groups changes nothing under the adaptive schedule, which rewrites every group each minibatch from `self.learning_rate` (`copt_ppo.py:217-230`), and nothing under a fixed schedule, whose groups already hold the initial rate.

D6's remedy stands and its mechanism is restated (finding 10). The swap's reset evaluates each curriculum term a second time at the same step counter, on every environment's truncated sum or on the last step's termination buffers, so a term whose interval divides the swap step is evaluated twice there and, for the push force, stepped twice. The quadruped's swap step is 480 x 25 = 12000, which the intervals 6000, 7200, 9600 and 48000 of its configuration meet every 1, 3, 4 and 4 swaps. Suppressing the terms across the swap's reset leaves the evaluation on the completed episodes of the step, which is the one the curriculum was designed around. The flag is read by six functions, five of which both copt tasks call, and the sixth, `modify_push_force_v2`, only the SD_BRS1 tasks call (`brs_base_env_cfg.py:932`), where the flag is never set, so it is gated too and nothing changes for it.

The second D6 defect, intervals written as iterations x 24 in a task whose runner takes 25 steps per iteration, holds for the biped's copt task as well as the quadruped's (finding 18), and is corrected in both copt tasks alone, each robot's `CurriculumCfg` being shared with its plain tasks. Each robot's file gains `CoptCurriculumCfg(CurriculumCfg)`, which redefines the seven terms in iterations x 25, `terrain_levels`' starting step included, which the audit's count of six omitted, and the copt environment names it, the pattern of phase 6's reward classes. For the quadruped the class reproduces the second draft's `__post_init__` rescale term for term (section 9.6).

### 8.2 Architecture and rationale

| item | design | rationale |
|---|---|---|
| D2.1 | the biped's `obs_groups` on the quadruped copt runner | the audit's decision, the two robots then differ in robot and reward set only |
| D2.2 | `CoptPPO(estimator_loss_coef=1.0)`, set in `train.py`'s copt branch | default reproduces the unweighted sum, the plain runners never see the keyword |
| D2.3 | `robot_mass` and `robot_inertia` index `asset_cfg.body_ids`, the quadruped's morphology group names the housings and the thigh and shank links, every body a search moves | `body_ids` is `slice(None)` for every existing caller (`scene_entity_cfg.py:78`), so 13 call sites are unchanged, and the trunk's and feet's 50 constant channels are dropped |
| D5 | `copt.reinit_lr_each_generation`, default True, gates the random phase reset, the dict dumped as `copt_agent.yaml`, the reset written through | the audit's decision, the dump added by finding 8 |
| D6 | a `_suppress_curriculum` flag read by the six interval gates, raised by the runner around every swap reset and by `restore_env_state` | an attribute no other caller sets, so the gate is inert outside a copt run, rule 4 |
| D6 | `CoptCurriculumCfg` in each robot's file, its intervals x 25, named by the copt environment | the second review's item 1, and the plain tasks keep their schedule |

The `privilegedDynamicsObs` group stays defined on the quadruped. Its four terms are also critic terms and its 48 columns are computed whether or not an observation set reads them, as on the biped, which keeps the former arrangement one line away.


### 8.4 Implementation instructions

1. D2, apply the `obs_groups` hunk, the `CoptPPO` keyword and its use, the two `observations.py` hunks, and phase 6's morphology group hunk.
2. D5, the flag's read in the runner's constructor, the `elif`, the write-through in `reinit_learning_rate`, and the `copt_agent.yaml` dump. The flag and the coefficient themselves are in phase 1's `train.py` hunk.
3. D6, the six gates, the flag pairs in the runner and in `restore_env_state`, and both robots' `CoptCurriculumCfg`.

### 8.5 Verification and outcome

| check | result |
|---|---|
| quadruped estimator regresses `morphologyObs` from history | every quadruped run built `Estimator MLP` with 5875 inputs and 216 outputs, an actor of 264 inputs and a critic of 6690, section 9.4 |
| the biped's architecture unchanged but for the target width | 5575 inputs, 160 outputs, actor 196 inputs, critic 4890 |
| `copt_agent.yaml` carries the flag and the coefficient | `reinit_lr_each_generation: true`, `estimator_loss_coef: 1.0` in every copt run |
| `robot_mass` and `robot_inertia` unchanged for every caller that names no body | `body_ids` is `slice(None)` and the indexing is the identity, read, and section 9.6 |
| the curriculum gates are inert outside a copt run | the flag is set only by `CoptOnPolicyRunner` and `restore_env_state`, `grep -rn "_suppress_curriculum"` |

The outcome is a quadruped trained in the deployable arrangement the biped already uses, against a target whose every channel belongs to a body a search moves, a learning rate schedule whose resets are recorded in the run's own parameters, and curricula evaluated once per interval on completed episodes.

| D2.2, `estimator_loss_coef` defaulting to 1.0 in `CoptPPO`, multiplying the estimation loss (section 5) | identical, set explicitly in `train.py` | adhered |

## 9. Phase 8, verification record, order of application and the validation run

Every result below was produced against the sandbox, a copy of the working tree carrying every edit of phases 1 to 7, on 2026-10-06 and, for this revision, again on 2026-10-07. On 2026-10-06 the container's runs shared the GPU with a user's KScale training job, so their timings mean nothing and were not recorded. On 2026-10-07 the GPU was otherwise idle, and the timings given are from that day.

### 9.1 Host numerical checks

| check | quadruped | biped |
|---|---|---|
| `Family.chain` against forward kinematics of the emitted document, 400 random designs, foot lead | 5.8e-7 m | 1.1e-6 m |
| the same, foot depth | 2.5e-7 m | 3.6e-7 m |
| the donor's foot ahead of the hip, forward kinematics of the committed document | -0.0001 mm | +13.9868 mm |
| the donor's standing height, the same | 0.292000 m | 0.804361 m |
| `joint_rot` payload against the document's body quats | 5.0e-7 | 5.0e-7 |
| designs refused of 3000 random draws | 0 | 0 |

### 9.2 Importer and physics, appendices A and C

Appendix A takes two designs per robot, A with scales 1.2 and 0.85, lean +0.06 and knee -0.2 rad and the donor's actuators, and B with scales 0.8 and 1.15, lean -0.08 and knee +0.22 rad and other actuators on every joint, the quadruped's abduction and hip on `A1-KNEE` and its knee on `A1-ABAD`, the biped's abduction and knee on `TRON1-ANKLE`, its hip on `TRON1-KNEE` and its ankle on `TRON1-HIP`, which changes three of its cylinders, two placements, its sole and every housing mass. It converts both by `MjcfConverter` with `usd_generator`'s settings, spawns A in environments 0 and 1, which reference one USD, and B in environment 2, measures A at zero joint angles, then writes B in place through environment 0 alone with `apply_link_length_params`, runs `assert_link_lengths_applied`, and compares environments 0 and 1 with environment 2.

| quantity | quadruped | biped |
|---|---|---|
| pose joints read back | `thigh_{leg}_Link` fixed and `{leg}_calf_joint` revolute, 8 | `thigh_{S}_Link` and `ankle_{S}_act` fixed, `knee_{S}_Joint` revolute, 6 |
| `localRot1` of the revolute joints | (0.70711, 0, 0, 0.70711), +90 deg about z, axis (0, 1, 0) to x | (0.70711, 0, 0, -0.70711), -90 deg about z, axis (0, -1, 0) to x |
| `localRot1` of the fixed joints | identity | identity |
| worst error of `localRot0 = body quat x localRot1` | 5.4e-7 | 4.8e-7 |
| A spawned, depth and lead against its record, both environments | 4.7e-7 m | 6.2e-7 m |
| B in place, its actuators changed, depth and lead against its record, all three environments | 3.7e-7 m | 3.1e-7 m |

Written in place, B is its own conversion. Each row is the worst difference over every body of environments 0 and 1 against environment 2, and the last column the smallest difference A had from B in that quantity before the write, which shows the comparison could see a missed edit.

| quantity | quadruped, after | biped, after | before the write, quadruped and biped |
|---|---|---|---|
| body position relative to the root, zero joint angles | 7.2e-7 m | 3.7e-7 m | 7.4e-2 and 1.0e-1 m |
| body orientation, 1 less the absolute quaternion dot product | 1.8e-7 | 1.2e-7 | 3.1e-2 and 1.2e-2 |
| mass, the PhysX articulation view | 2.4e-7 kg | 3.6e-7 kg | 0.41 and 0.60 kg |
| inertia, the same | 4.8e-7 kg m^2 | 5.0e-7 kg m^2 | 9.4e-4 and 9.6e-3 kg m^2 |
| centre of mass, the same | 0 | 0 | 4.5e-2 and 6.0e-2 m |
| collider volume as PhysX cooked it, `omni.physx` property query | 0 | 5.2e-10 m^3 | 7.2e-5 and 2.5e-4 m^3 |
| `assert_link_lengths_applied`, every placement and mass of the payload in environments 0 and 1 | passed | passed | |

The observation read the joint frames of the same environments.

| quantity | quadruped | biped |
|---|---|---|
| bodies read | `thigh_{leg}_Link`, `shank_{leg}_Link`, 8 | `thigh_{S}_Link`, `shank_{S}_Link`, `ankle_{S}_act`, 6 |
| design A's fold, thigh and shank, radians about +y | 0.77734, -1.96867 | 0.62687, -1.50900, ankle 0.88220 |
| against `pose_frames` before the write, environments 0 to 2 | 1.1e-6 rad | 1.1e-6 rad |
| against `pose_frames(B)` after the write, re-read through the new PhysX view | 9.5e-7 rad | 1.0e-6 rad |
| against the fold the bodies realise at zero joint angles, `2 atan2(y, w)` of the parent to child quaternion | 1.2e-7 rad | 1.2e-7 rad |
| 1024 new references to A's file, whose layer the write changed, against `pose_frames(B)` | 1.2e-7 rad | 1.2e-7 rad |
| stage read of those 1024 environments | 0.65 s | 0.40 s |

The 1e-6 residual is a converted document's, whose quaternions the builders write to six decimals (`_quat_y`), the in place writes agreeing to 1.2e-7.

The revolute `localRot0` of design A's quadruped knee, (0.39132, -0.58895, -0.58895, 0.39132), is its body quat, a rotation of -1.9687 rad about +y, composed with the alignment, and not the body quat, which is the measurement behind section 1's finding 1.

| joints after 200 steps | 1.5e-5 rad | 8.3e-7 rad |

### 9.3 Unit suite

`co_optimisation/tests/design_generator_test.py` in the container passed 66 of 66, both with the suite's `cmaes` stub and with the real `cmaes` 0.13.1 installed to a private directory, against 43 of 43 for the current tree. The suite gained 29 cases, the range test, the two catalogue tests, ten nominal pose tests over both families, the payload test run over the categorical random phase as well and the test that the payload covers everything a pick moves, and lost the six cases of the three shortlist tests, the dimension test being rewritten in place.

### 9.4 End to end training runs

`train.py` from the sandbox, 256 environments, 32 CMA-ES designs, a random phase of `256 / 4 = 64` designs, a generation every 2 iterations, the late start at iteration 4, 9 iterations and a checkpoint every 4, for every generator on both robots.

```zsh
./isaaclab.sh -p biped/scripts/rsl_rl/train.py --task Isaac-Quadruped-Copt-Rough-v0 --policy-type COPT --design_search length --headless \
    --num_envs 256 --copt_num_individuals 32 --ea_update_interval 2 --ea_late_start 4 --max_iterations 9 --save_interval 4
```

The `length` runs exercise every swap route, the measured standing heights and the worst disagreement with the records being printed at each. The table is the 2026-10-07 runs, whose first three swaps repeat those of 2026-10-06 exactly, the CMA-ES generations differing as their fitness does, the networks having changed width.

| swap | iteration | route | quadruped standing heights, worst disagreement | biped standing heights, worst disagreement |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| bootstrap, 64 random designs at 5 per cent of the box | 0 | respawn | [0.2862, 0.2965] m, 0.004 mm | [0.8019, 0.8067] m, 0.005 mm |
| random generation 1 | 2 | in place | [0.2507, 0.3294] m, 0.004 mm | [0.7350, 0.8604] m, 0.005 mm |
| late start, 32 CMA-ES designs | 4 | respawn | [0.2407, 0.3491] m, 0.004 mm | [0.7316, 0.9026] m, 0.006 mm |
| CMA-ES generation 2 | 6 | in place | [0.2328, 0.3411] m, 0.004 mm | [0.7262, 0.9008] m, 0.003 mm |
| CMA-ES generation 3 | 8 | in place | [0.2481, 0.3489] m, 0.004 mm | [0.6863, 0.8728] m, 0.004 mm |

The other generators ran the same schedule, on 2026-10-06 and again on 2026-10-07, and every run below ended with exit 0 and wrote `model_0.pt`, `model_4.pt` and `model_8.pt`. The heights are the 2026-10-07 runs'.

| run | routes | standing heights over the run | worst disagreement |
|---|---|---|---|
| quadruped `cmaes` | respawn, in place, late start respawn, two in place | [0.2157, 0.3752] m | 0.004 mm |
| biped `cmaes` | the same | [0.6794, 0.9338] m | 0.006 mm |
| quadruped `cat` | respawn, in place, late start respawn, two in place | [0.2507, 0.3294] m in the random phase, [0.2849, 0.2991] m searched | 0.004 mm |
| biped `cat` | the same | [0.7350, 0.8787] m in the random phase, [0.7820, 0.8339] m searched | 0.005 mm |
| quadruped `random` | a respawn of 32 designs, then four in place | [0.2089, 0.3688] m | 0.004 mm |
| biped `random` | the same | [0.6604, 0.9275] m | 0.006 mm |

The categorical runs of 2026-10-07 wrote every generation but the bootstrap and the late start in place, where those of 2026-10-06 respawned all five, and their logs time both routes on the same 256 environments. A respawn spent 7.4 to 14.0 s spawning on the quadruped and 5.5 to 10.0 s on the biped before its `sim.reset()`, of 7.3 to 8.3 s and 6.1 to 6.3 s, while an in place generation spent its `sim.reset()` alone, 7.1 to 7.6 s and 5.8 to 6.5 s, the authoring itself not printed, so the in place route saves the spawn, 47 to 63 per cent of a respawn's time at this size.

The categorical random phase drew every joint's actuator from the whole shelf. Counted by the torque limit each design's document states per joint class, the 64 designs of generation zero used every torque class of the shelf on every joint of both robots, `TRON1-ABAD` and `TRON1-HIP` sharing 60 N m and `A1-ABAD` and `A1-HIP` 23.6 N m, and the unit test of section 9.3 checks the names themselves. The narrow searched bands of the categorical runs are section 1 finding 16, the strategy's initial spread being about a twelfth of the other searches'.

| further run | result |
|---|---|
| each robot's `length` run resumed from its `model_4.pt` for four iterations, 2026-10-06 | the checkpointed population re-measured at exactly the heights it was saved with, [0.2407, 0.3491] m and [0.7316, 0.9026] m, the environment state restored, and two further in place swaps passed |
| each robot's `cat` run resumed from its `model_4.pt` for four iterations, 2026-10-07 | the checkpointed categorical population written in place over the respawned one, re-measured at exactly its saved heights, [0.2861, 0.2965] m and [0.7820, 0.8339] m, and two further categorical generations written in place, within 0.004 mm |
| `Isaac-Quadruped-Copt-Flat-v0` and `Isaac-Limx-SF-Copt-MoRAL-Flat-v0`, one iteration, a save every iteration | `model_0.pt` written on the plane by both, B2, the bootstrap standing within 0.002 and 0.003 mm |
| the quadruped `length` run with `--num_envs 250` | refused before the runner was built, `ValueError: --num_envs 250 is not a multiple of the 62 designs a generation of --design_search length carries.` |
| `Isaac-Quadruped-Blind-Rough-v0` and `Isaac-Quadruped-HIM-Blind-Rough-v0`, two iterations on the regenerated quadruped document | trained without error, the first on both dates, the second on 2026-10-06 |
| `Isaac-Limx-SF-Identified-Blind-Rough-v0`, two iterations on the regenerated biped document | trained without error on both dates, its critic's None `nominal_pose` skipped |

Every copt run passed the ordering, zero nominal and mirror row assertions at every swap, every CMA-ES strategy printed `aCMA-ES ... in dimension 4`, the quadruped built an actor of 264 inputs, a critic of 6690 and an estimator of 5875 inputs and 216 outputs and the biped 196, 4890, 5575 and 160, and every run directory carries `params/copt_agent.yaml` with `reinit_lr_each_generation: true` and `estimator_loss_coef: 1.0`.

### 9.6 Every task's configuration, before and after

Every registered task's environment and agent configuration was serialised with `class_to_dict` and compared field by field, from the working tree as it stood on 2026-10-07, from this revision's sandbox, and from the second draft's sandbox. All 66 tasks load in all three. Against the tree, forty differ in nothing but the absolute path prefix of their asset files, which is where the sandbox sat, after one correction. The ten KScale tasks first differed in `rewards/pen_base_height/weight`, -60.0 in the tree and -30.0 in the sandbox, because `cfg/SF/kscale_base_env_cfg.py` was edited in the tree at 05:10 on 2026-10-07, after the sandbox was copied. This plan touches no line of that file, and the sandbox's copy was refreshed from the tree. The twelve plain SF tasks differ only in `observations/critic/nominal_pose`, a new field whose None the observation manager skips. The eight plain and HIM quadruped tasks, Flat and Rough with their Play variants, differ in exactly two fields, `scene/robot/init_state/joint_pos` of `.._thigh_joint` from 0.884337 to 0.0 and of `.._calf_joint` from -1.768673 to 0.0. The six co-optimisation tasks differ in the fields this plan sets, listed for the quadruped's rough task.

| field | before | after |
|---|---|---|
| `scene/num_envs` | 6000 | 6144 |
| `scene/robot/init_state/joint_pos` thigh and calf | 0.884337, -1.768673 | 0.0, 0.0 |
| `rewards/pen_base_height/func` | `base_height_rough_l2` | `base_height_rough_l2_v2` |
| `rewards/rew_foot_clearance/func` | `foot_clearance_reward_v4` | `foot_clearance_reward_v6`, `target_height_frac` added |
| `rewards/feet_air_time/func` | `feet_air_time_v2` | `feet_air_time_v3`, `reference_height` added |
| `observations/morphologyObs` | lengths, mass, inertia over all bodies | lengths, mass and inertia over the housings and the thigh and shank links, `nominal_pose` added |
| `observations/critic` | | `nominal_pose` added |
| `curriculum`, seven terms | intervals and starting steps x 24 | x 25 |
| agent `obs_groups` | `policy` with `morphologyObs`, `encoderIn` with `morphologyObs`, target `privilegedDynamicsObs` | `policy`, `historyObs`, target `morphologyObs` |

The biped's three copt tasks differ in the base height and regulation targets (0.75 to 0.804), the foot radius (0.03 to 0.05), the three reward functions, the `nominal_pose` terms of the morphology group and the critic, and the seven curriculum terms from x 24 to x 25, and in nothing else. The plain biped tasks' configurations are otherwise unchanged, the change to their robot being in the regenerated document they load.

Against the second draft, the comparison that isolates this revision, 48 tasks are identical, the twelve plain SF tasks gain the critic's None field, the three quadruped copt tasks differ in the morphology group's body list and the two `nominal_pose` terms, and the three biped copt tasks in the two `nominal_pose` terms and the curriculum. Every copt task's `rewards` is identical to the second draft's, so the reward classes reproduce the `__post_init__` wiring term for term, and the run logs' reward manager tables list the terms in the order of the shared `RewardsCfg`, `pen_base_height` at index 3 of the quadruped's 21 terms and 5 of the biped's 20, a field redefined in a configclass keeping its parent's position.

### 9.7 Order of application

The phases are reading units. Application follows the files, each edited once, in an order that keeps the tree importable at every step.

1. In the training container, reinstall both packages editable, `./isaaclab.sh -p -m pip install -e biped/design_generator biped/co_optimisation`, which also installs `cmaes`. Confirm with `./isaaclab.sh -p -c "import cma, cmaes, pandas, design_generator; print(design_generator.__file__)"`, which must print the repository's path and not `site-packages`.
2. `design_generator`, phase 3's constants, `GEOMETRY_RULES.md`, both `generate.py`, both builders, then phase 2's catalogue function, both `actuator.py` and both `actuators.json`.
3. Keep today's two committed documents and regenerate them, section 4.4 steps 4 and 5, running appendix C once both are written.
4. `co_optimisation/co_optimisation/runners/usd_generator.py`, every hunk of phases 1, 2, 4 and 5 in one pass.
5. `co_optimisation/co_optimisation/utils/update.py` and `env_state.py`, `co_optimisation/co_optimisation/algorithms/copt_ppo.py`, `co_optimisation/co_optimisation/runners/copt_on_policy_runner.py`, `co_optimisation/setup.py`.
6. `environments`, `mdp/rewards.py`, `mdp/observations.py`, `mdp/curriculums.py`, then `assets/config/quadruped_identified_cfg.py`, `robots/quadruped_pointfoot_env_cfg.py`, `cfg/quadruped/base_env_cfg.py`, `cfg/SF/limx_base_env_cfg.py`, `agents/quadruped_rsl_rl_ppo_cfg.py`.
7. `scripts/rsl_rl/cli_args.py`, `scripts/rsl_rl/train.py`, `README.md`.
8. The tests, the modified `design_generator_test.py` and the three new `sim_*_check.py` scripts of appendices A to C.
9. Run the unit suite, `./isaaclab.sh -p -m pytest -q co_optimisation/tests/design_generator_test.py` from `biped/`, then appendices A and B, then the validation run.

### 9.8 The validation run

The audit's order of work makes a short run of both robots the gate before any long run. It is the runs of section 9.4 repeated on the real tree, with logs kept out of the user's log tree by running from a scratch directory.

```zsh
cd /tmp/copt_validation
SCHED="--num_envs 256 --copt_num_individuals 32 --ea_update_interval 2 --ea_late_start 4"
# every generator on both robots, every swap route, a save every 4 iterations
for search in length cmaes cat random; do
  for task in Isaac-Quadruped-Copt-Rough-v0 Isaac-Limx-SF-Copt-MoRAL-Rough-v0; do
    /workspace/isaaclab/isaaclab.sh -p /workspace/isaaclab/biped/scripts/rsl_rl/train.py --task $task --policy-type COPT \
        --design_search $search $SCHED --max_iterations 9 --save_interval 4 --headless
  done
done
# a resume of each robot's length and cat runs from their model_4.pt
/workspace/isaaclab/isaaclab.sh -p /workspace/isaaclab/biped/scripts/rsl_rl/train.py --task <task> --policy-type COPT $SCHED \
    --max_iterations 4 --save_interval 4 --resume True --checkpoint_path <run>/model_4.pt --headless
# B2, a save on a plane, and the plain tasks on the regenerated assets
for task in Isaac-Quadruped-Copt-Flat-v0 Isaac-Limx-SF-Copt-MoRAL-Flat-v0; do
  /workspace/isaaclab/isaaclab.sh -p /workspace/isaaclab/biped/scripts/rsl_rl/train.py --task $task --policy-type COPT \
      $SCHED --max_iterations 1 --save_interval 1 --headless
done
/workspace/isaaclab/isaaclab.sh -p /workspace/isaaclab/biped/scripts/rsl_rl/train.py --task Isaac-Quadruped-Blind-Rough-v0 --num_envs 256 --max_iterations 2 --headless
/workspace/isaaclab/isaaclab.sh -p /workspace/isaaclab/biped/scripts/rsl_rl/train.py --task Isaac-Limx-SF-Identified-Blind-Rough-v0 --num_envs 256 --max_iterations 2 --headless
```

| pass criterion | where it shows |
|---|---|
| `[copt] nominal pose measured ... worst disagreement` under 2 mm at every swap, any larger value raising | the run log |
| no assertion from the ordering, zero nominal or mirror row checks | the run log |
| `aCMA-ES ... in dimension 4` for `length` and `cmaes` | the run log |
| `Estimator MLP` output 216 for the quadruped and 160 for the biped | the run log |
| every categorical generation but the late start written in place, `Applying link length parameters in place` | the run log |
| `params/copt_agent.yaml` with `reinit_lr_each_generation: true` and `estimator_loss_coef: 1.0` | the run directory |
| a resumed run measures the checkpointed heights exactly, a categorical one written in place | the resumed log against the original's |
| the categorical search picks only `A1-*` or `TRON1-*` names, every one of them in its random phase | `designs/link_lengths_*.csv` and the specs printed by the generator |
| `model_0.pt` written on the plane | the flat run directory |

### 9.9 Expected outcome

After application, `train.py --policy-type COPT` on either robot's copt task builds that robot's designs with any of the four generators, searches four parameters, two link lengths, a hip lean and a knee bend, with the biped's ankle keeping its sole flat, and with the categorical search the actuator of every joint after a random phase over all of them, every generation but the late start written in place. Every design's fold lives in its body frames as the committed assets' now does, every environment's pose is measured at every swap, each design spawns just above its own standing height, is rewarded against its own height and stride timing, and a deployable estimator infers the morphology and the fold, which it and the critic read from the joint frames the document folded into the articulation, from history. The first long run is then interpretable, which the audit's order of work names as the condition for steps 8 to 11.
