## 10. Defects left standing, compatibility and what this plan does not do

### 10.1 Defects left standing, each recorded where it lives

| defect | where | why it stands |
|---|---|---|
| A design's absolute pitch envelope moves with its own offset, the joint ranges being shifted by the constants' fold alone, so a design leaning 0.5 rad has its hardware stops 0.5 rad from where the hardware has them | both builders' `_leg_xml` | the audit's choice, keeping the in place editor free of joint limits, and the check of phase 4 keeps every nominal inside the narrowed hardware range |
| `feet_regulation_v2`'s `foot_radius` is the TRON1 ankle's 0.050, while a categorical pick of another ankle housing puts the sole up to 0.068 m below the foot body | `cfg/SF/limx_base_env_cfg.py`, `CoptRewardsCfg` | a configuration constant, as before, the per design sole being available in the record if a later pass wants it |
| `feet_regulation` and its v2 read the absolute foot height, not the height above the terrain | `rewards.py:1963-1965`, the original's own TODO | unchanged by this plan |
| the original five reward functions keep pricing a design's geometry when called with a fixed target | the docstring of each v2 records it | rule 6, the other configurations opt in deliberately |
| the plain quadruped and plain SF biped tasks' curricula still run at x 24 against 25 steps per iteration | `cfg/quadruped/base_env_cfg.py:1215-1296`, `cfg/SF/limx_base_env_cfg.py:1043-1125` | the shared `CurriculumCfg` of each robot, each copt task taking its own `CoptCurriculumCfg` |
| the quadruped's unit scale design is 0.21 m per link, not the donor's 0.213, the 2 dp rounding | `usd_generator.py`, `_spec_from_parameters` | D8, recorded by the audit and left by decision |
| the estimator target's channels remain unweighted | `copt_ppo.py`, the estimation loss | the per channel standardisation is the audit's further remedy, not its plan |
| `robot_mass` and the five other observation functions build their device from `torch.device("cuda")` | `observations.py:25-132` | H5, deferred, the two the plan touches keeping their device lines unchanged |
| the critic's `robot_joint_pos` reports `default_joint_pos`, now zero on both robots but for the startup noise, so its pitch channels carry almost nothing | `observations.py:70-76`, the plain, HIM and copt critics | a privileged constant of the critic, kept so every critic's width and layout are unchanged, and superseded in the copt estimator target by `robot_nominal_pose` |
| `randomize_joint_default_pos`'s startup noise does not survive a respawn or an in place update, `default_joint_pos` being rebuilt from the configuration at every initialisation | `articulation.py:1666-1687` against `mdp/events.py:247-283` | true before this plan for every copt run, and the zero nominal assertion of phase 5 relies on it |
| `scripts/rsl_rl/play.py:1083` hard codes `"biped"` | play's copt branch | harmless, play never generates a design and loads a CMA-ES state that is family agnostic |
| `CatCMAwM`'s initial spread is about 0.021 in the unit box against the 0.25 the other searches start at | `usd_generator.py`, `CatCMAESDesignGenerator._make_strategy` | finding 16, a decision of section 11 |
| a pick's peak torque reaches the run through the actuator model's `effort_limit` alone, the PhysX joint limit being the configuration's `effort_limit_sim` whichever route a design arrives by | `respawn.py`, `apply_actuator_params`, against `articulation.py:1772-1773` | X3, deferred by the audit, and identical on the respawn and the in place route, Isaac Lab writing the configuration's limits at every initialisation |
| the importer authors a cylinder's `extent` about x while its `axis` is z, so a housing's bounds are those of a cylinder turned by 90 deg | `MjcfUsd.cpp:813-815` | an importer defect, the editor reproducing it so that a written design equals a converted one, and the bounds feeding no physics, the collider being cooked from `radius`, `height` and `axis` |

### 10.2 Compatibility

| caller | effect |
|---|---|
| the plain and HIM quadruped tasks, Flat and Rough with their Play variants | physically the same robot (section 9.2), the pitch joints measured from the crouch rather than from the straight leg, so the actor's observations and actions are unchanged while the critic's `robot_joint_pos` channels and the joint positions in a run's dumps shift by the fold (section 4.1). A policy trained before the change acts identically after it, and a resumed run's value function sees eight shifted constants |
| the plain biped tasks on `base_robot.xml` | physically the same robot and every joint coordinate unchanged, the thigh's own frame turned by 35.0 deg, so the critics that read `robot_inertia` see the two thighs' channels in the new frame |
| the KScale, SD_BRS1, Go1 and every task outside these two assets | none, section 9.6 |
| the MuJoCo documents and the model predictive control package that reads them | the biped's document unchanged, the quadruped's home keyframe moved to the trained crouch by D1a |
| `play.py` on a copt task | unchanged in form, its `DEFAULT_PARAM_RANGES` entries typed and its generator four dimensional |
| a co-optimisation checkpoint written before this plan | refused, by `_check_dimension`, two dimensions against four, and by the actor's input width, the estimator output having widened, so such a checkpoint is played or resumed from the commit that trained it |
| a caller passing a retired, unknown or `absolute` typed parameter | raises at construction, where it was silently ignored |
| a caller passing an untyped `(lo, hi)` | unchanged, read as a scale |
| a caller passing `catalog_names` or `actuator_groups` | raises `TypeError`, the arguments being deleted by B6 |
| `--design_search length` or `cat`, or no flag | `length` unchanged, `cat` now runs, with a random phase, and swaps in place |
| the plain SF tasks | their critic gains a `nominal_pose` field defaulting to None, which the observation manager skips, so the dumped `params/env.yaml` gains one null key and the observation is unchanged |
| the copt tasks' critics | the nominal pose joins `link_lengths`, 8 channels per step for the quadruped and 6 for the biped over the ten step history |
| a search over the lengths alone | narrow both offset ranges to `("offset", (0.0, 0.0))`, which keeps the knee at the donor's fold and the hip at the angle that puts the foot under the hip, `test_zero_width_pose_ranges_search_the_lengths_alone` |

### 10.3 Untouched by decision

Record items D3, D4, D8 and X8, and the deferred L1 to L4, X1 to X4, X6, X7 and H1 to H5, stand as the audit records them. X6 changes in kind rather than by edit. `check_geometry` still validates only the trunk width, and the fallback it guarded is now reachable through `check_nominal_pose`, which is the use the audit intended for it.

| the categorical random phase's actuator draw | uniform over the shelf from the first generation | `CatCMAESDesignGenerator._generate_individual`, for instance keeping the donor's pick with the probability `1 - scale` the continuous prior uses |

## Appendix A, `co_optimisation/tests/sim_nominal_pose_check.py`

A new file, run with Isaac Sim as its docstring states and not collected by pytest, whose name matches neither of pytest's default patterns. It covers the nominal pose of phases 3 to 5, the categorical in place edit of phase 2 and the observation of phase 6. Section 9.2 records its output.

```python
"""Importer and physics check of the nominal pose, the in place editor and the nominal pose observation, run with Isaac Sim, not collected by pytest.

For each family it builds two designs A and B that differ in their lengths, their pose
and every actuator they pick, converts them with the MJCF importer exactly as
usd_generator does, and then

1. reads every pose joint's physics:localRot0 and localRot1 back from A's converted USD
   and checks localRot0 == body quat * localRot1, the rule update.py applies,
2. spawns A in environments 0 and 1 and B in environment 2, B as a respawn makes it,
   measures A's foot depth and foot lead over the hip pitch axis at zero joint angles
   against Family.nominal(A), and reads the nominal pose observation,
3. writes B in place through environment 0 with apply_link_length_params, and compares
   environments 0 and 1, the second never written, with environment 2 in every body's
   pose at zero joint angles, every body's mass, inertia and centre of mass as PhysX
   holds them and every collider's volume as PhysX cooked it, runs
   assert_link_lengths_applied, and measures the pose against Family.nominal(B),
4. reads the observation again, which must have re-read the frames, against
   pose_frames(B) and against the fold the bodies realise at zero joint angles,
5. times the observation's stage read over 1024 new references to A's file, whose layer
   the write of step 3 changed, so that they read B.

    ./isaaclab.sh -p biped/co_optimisation/tests/sim_nominal_pose_check.py [quadruped|biped]

plans/co_optimisation_audit_implementation.md section 9.2 records its output.
"""
import os
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import types

from isaaclab.app import AppLauncher

app = AppLauncher(headless=True).app

import numpy as np  # noqa: E402
import torch  # noqa: E402
from omni.physx import get_physx_property_query_interface  # noqa: E402
from omni.physx.bindings._physx import PhysxPropertyQueryMode  # noqa: E402
from pxr import Gf, PhysicsSchemaTools, Usd, UsdUtils  # noqa: E402

import isaaclab.sim as sim_utils  # noqa: E402
from isaaclab.actuators import ImplicitActuatorCfg  # noqa: E402
from isaaclab.assets import Articulation, ArticulationCfg  # noqa: E402
from isaaclab.managers import ObservationTermCfg, SceneEntityCfg  # noqa: E402
from isaaclab.sim.converters import MjcfConverter, MjcfConverterCfg  # noqa: E402

import environments.tasks.locomotion.mdp as mdp  # noqa: E402
from co_optimisation.runners import usd_generator as U  # noqa: E402
from co_optimisation.utils import update as UP  # noqa: E402

OUT = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="copt_pose_")
ROOT_PRIM = {"quadruped": "/trunk/trunk", "biped": "/base_Link/base_Link"}
PICKS_B = {"quadruped": {"abad": "A1-KNEE", "hip": "A1-KNEE", "knee": "A1-ABAD"},
           "biped": {"abad": "TRON1-ANKLE", "hip": "TRON1-KNEE", "knee": "TRON1-ANKLE", "ankle": "TRON1-HIP"}}
FOLDED = {"quadruped": ["thigh_.*_Link", "shank_.*_Link"],
          "biped": ["thigh_.*_Link", "shank_.*_Link", "ankle_.*_act"]}
PARENT = {"thigh": "knee_{}_act", "shank": "thigh_{}_Link", "ankle": "shank_{}_Link"}
results = []


def convert(fam, spec, tag):
    xml_path = os.path.join(OUT, f"{fam.name}_{tag}.xml")
    with open(xml_path, "w") as fh:
        fh.write(fam.build_xml(spec))
    cfg = MjcfConverterCfg(asset_path=xml_path, usd_dir=os.path.join(OUT, f"{fam.name}_{tag}_usd"),
                           usd_file_name=f"{fam.name}_{tag}.usd", link_density=0.0,
                           import_inertia_tensor=True, fix_base=False, self_collision=False,
                           make_instanceable=True, force_usd_conversion=True)
    return MjcfConverter(cfg).usd_path


def check_joint_frames(fam, spec, usd_path):
    stage = Usd.Stage.Open(usd_path)
    joints = {p.GetName(): p for p in stage.Traverse() if p.HasAttribute("physics:localRot0")}
    worst = 0.0
    for link, entry in fam.links(spec).items():
        for joint, q in entry.get("joint_rot", {}).items():
            prim = joints[joint]
            r0 = prim.GetAttribute("physics:localRot0").Get()
            r1 = prim.GetAttribute("physics:localRot1").Get()
            want = Gf.Quatf(*q) * r1
            d = min(max(abs(a - b) for a, b in zip((r0.GetReal(), *r0.GetImaginary()), (want.GetReal(), *want.GetImaginary()))),
                    max(abs(a + b) for a, b in zip((r0.GetReal(), *r0.GetImaginary()), (want.GetReal(), *want.GetImaginary()))))
            worst = max(worst, d)
    return worst


def zero_pose(robot):
    robot.write_joint_state_to_sim(torch.zeros_like(robot.data.default_joint_pos),
                                   torch.zeros_like(robot.data.default_joint_vel))
    pose = robot.data.default_root_state[:, :7].clone()
    pose[:, 0] = torch.arange(robot.num_instances, device=pose.device, dtype=pose.dtype) * 3.0
    pose[:, 1] = 0.0
    pose[:, 2] = 2.0
    pose[:, 3:7] = torch.tensor([1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0], device=pose.device)
    robot.write_root_pose_to_sim(pose)
    return pose[:, :3]


def measure(robot, fam):
    zero_pose(robot)
    foot_ids, _ = robot.find_bodies(list(fam.foot_bodies), preserve_order=True)
    hip_ids, _ = robot.find_bodies(list(fam.hip_bodies), preserve_order=True)
    p = robot.data.body_link_pos_w
    return (2.0 - p[:, foot_ids, 2]).cpu().numpy(), (p[:, foot_ids, 0] - p[:, hip_ids, 0]).cpu().numpy()


def collider_volumes(stage, robot, root):
    stage_id = UsdUtils.StageCache.Get().Insert(stage).ToLongInt()
    out = np.zeros((robot.num_instances, robot.num_bodies))
    for i in range(robot.num_instances):
        for k, body in enumerate(robot.body_names):
            path = f"/World/envs/env_{i}/Robot/{root}/{body}"
            vol = []
            get_physx_property_query_interface().query_prim(
                stage_id=stage_id, prim_id=PhysicsSchemaTools.sdfPathToInt(path),
                query_mode=PhysxPropertyQueryMode.QUERY_RIGID_BODY_WITH_COLLIDERS, timeout_ms=2000,
                rigid_body_fn=lambda r: None, collider_fn=lambda r: vol.append(r.volume), finished_fn=lambda: None)
            out[i, k] = sum(vol)
    return out


def physics_state(robot, stage, root):
    origin = zero_pose(robot)
    view = robot.root_physx_view
    return {
        "body position": (robot.data.body_link_pos_w - origin[:, None, :]).cpu().numpy(),
        "body orientation": robot.data.body_link_quat_w.cpu().numpy(),
        "mass": view.get_masses().cpu().numpy(),
        "inertia": view.get_inertias().cpu().numpy(),
        "centre of mass": view.get_coms().cpu().numpy()[..., :3],
        "collider volume": collider_volumes(stage, robot, root),
    }


class Scene(types.SimpleNamespace):
    def __getitem__(self, key):
        return self.articulations[key]


def stub_env(robot, paths, regex, sim=None):
    scene = Scene(env_prim_paths=paths, env_regex_ns=regex, articulations={"robot": robot})
    return types.SimpleNamespace(scene=scene, num_envs=len(paths), device="cuda:0", sim=sim)


def folds(fam, robot, spec_per_env):
    """The observation's expected value, pose_frames per environment's design, in articulation body order."""
    ids, names = robot.find_bodies(FOLDED[fam.name])
    want = [[fam.module.builder.pose_frames(s)[n.split("_")[0]] for n in names] for s in spec_per_env]
    return ids, names, torch.tensor(want)


def realised_folds(robot, names):
    zero_pose(robot)
    q = robot.data.body_link_quat_w.cpu()
    out = []
    for n in names:
        kind, side = n.split("_")[0], n.split("_")[1]
        c = q[:, robot.body_names.index(n)]
        p = q[:, robot.body_names.index(PARENT[kind].format(side))]
        w = p[:, 0] * c[:, 0] + (p[:, 1:] * c[:, 1:]).sum(-1)
        y = p[:, 0] * c[:, 2] - c[:, 0] * p[:, 2] - (p[:, 3] * c[:, 1] - p[:, 1] * c[:, 3])
        out.append(2.0 * torch.atan2(y, w))
    return torch.stack(out, 1)


FAMS = [a for a in sys.argv[1:] if a in ("quadruped", "biped")] or ["quadruped", "biped"]
for name in FAMS:
    gen = U.CMAESDesignGenerator(family=name, num_individuals=2, output_dir=OUT)
    fam = gen.family
    a = gen._spec_from_parameters({"thigh_length_scale": 1.2, "shank_length_scale": 0.85,
                                   "thigh_bend_offset": 0.06, "knee_bend_offset": -0.2})
    b = gen._spec_from_parameters({"thigh_length_scale": 0.8, "shank_length_scale": 1.15,
                                   "thigh_bend_offset": -0.08, "knee_bend_offset": 0.22}, PICKS_B[name])
    U.check_nominal_pose(fam, a, gen._dx_target, gen._lean_max())
    U.check_nominal_pose(fam, b, gen._dx_target, gen._lean_max())
    na, nb = fam.nominal(a), fam.nominal(b)
    print(f"\n==== {name}: A {a.actuators()[0].name}.. offsets {na['offsets']} height {na['height']:.4f}, "
          f"B picks {PICKS_B[name]} offsets {nb['offsets']} height {nb['height']:.4f}")
    usd_a, usd_b = convert(fam, a, "A"), convert(fam, b, "B")
    worst_frames = check_joint_frames(fam, a, usd_a)
    print(f" joint frames of A, worst localRot0 rule error {worst_frames:.2e}")

    sim_utils.create_new_stage()
    sim = sim_utils.SimulationContext(sim_utils.SimulationCfg(dt=0.005, device="cuda:0"))
    for i, usd in enumerate((usd_a, usd_a, usd_b)):
        sim_utils.create_prim(f"/World/envs/env_{i}", "Xform")
        sim_utils.UsdFileCfg(usd_path=usd).func(f"/World/envs/env_{i}/Robot", sim_utils.UsdFileCfg(usd_path=usd))
    robot = Articulation(ArticulationCfg(
        prim_path="/World/envs/env_.*/Robot", spawn=None,
        articulation_root_prim_path=ROOT_PRIM[name],
        actuators={"all": ImplicitActuatorCfg(joint_names_expr=[".*"], stiffness=0.0, damping=0.0)},
    ))
    sim.reset()
    stage = sim_utils.get_current_stage()
    root = fam.root_body
    paths = [f"/World/envs/env_{i}" for i in range(3)]
    env = stub_env(robot, paths, "/World/envs/env_.*", sim)

    depth, lead = measure(robot, fam)
    err_a = max(np.abs(depth[:2] - na["depth"]).max(), np.abs(lead[:2] - na["dx"]).max())
    print(f" A spawned: depth {depth[:2, 0]} want {na['depth']:.5f}, lead {lead[:2, 0]} want {na['dx']:.5f}, worst {err_a * 1e3:.3f} mm")

    ids, names, _ = folds(fam, robot, [a, a, b])
    asset_cfg = SceneEntityCfg("robot", body_names=FOLDED[name])
    asset_cfg.body_ids = ids
    term = mdp.robot_nominal_pose(ObservationTermCfg(func=mdp.robot_nominal_pose, params={"asset_cfg": asset_cfg}), env)
    obs = term(env, asset_cfg).cpu()
    obs_err_a = (obs - folds(fam, robot, [a, a, b])[2]).abs().max().item()
    print(f" observation over {names}: {np.round(obs.numpy(), 5).tolist()}, worst against pose_frames {obs_err_a:.2e} rad")

    before = physics_state(robot, stage, root)
    body_names, want_b = list(robot.body_names), folds(fam, robot, [b])[2]

    # B in place through environment 0 alone, the production path
    sim._disable_app_control_on_stop_handle = True
    sim.stop()
    sim._disable_app_control_on_stop_handle = False
    UP.apply_link_length_params(stub_env(robot, paths[:1], "/World/envs/env_.*", sim), [fam.links(b)], root)
    UP.assert_link_lengths_applied(env, [fam.links(b)])
    state = physics_state(robot, stage, root)

    def gap(value, rows):
        if value.ndim == 3 and value.shape[-1] == 4:
            return float((1.0 - np.abs((value[rows] * value[2:3]).sum(-1))).max())
        return float(np.abs(value[rows] - value[2:3]).max())

    diffs, moved = {}, {}
    print(" environments 0 (written) and 1 (never written) against environment 2 (B converted), A against B before the write:")
    for key in state:
        diffs[key], moved[key] = gap(state[key], slice(0, 2)), gap(before[key], slice(0, 1))
        print(f"   {key:18s} after {diffs[key]:.2e}   before {moved[key]:.2e}")
    depth, lead = measure(robot, fam)
    err_b = max(np.abs(depth - nb["depth"]).max(), np.abs(lead - nb["dx"]).max())
    print(f" B in place: depth {depth[:, 0]} want {nb['depth']:.5f}, lead {lead[:, 0]} want {nb['dx']:.5f}, worst {err_b * 1e3:.3f} mm")

    obs = term(env, asset_cfg).cpu()
    obs_err_b = (obs - want_b).abs().max().item()
    kin_err = (obs - realised_folds(robot, names)).abs().max().item()
    print(f" observation after the write: worst against pose_frames(B) {obs_err_b:.2e} rad, against the realised fold {kin_err:.2e} rad")

    # the stage read at scale, the cost every design swap pays once
    sim._disable_app_control_on_stop_handle = True
    sim.stop()
    sim._disable_app_control_on_stop_handle = False
    many = [f"/World/timing/env_{i}" for i in range(1024)]
    for p in many:
        sim_utils.UsdFileCfg(usd_path=usd_a).func(f"{p}/Robot", sim_utils.UsdFileCfg(usd_path=usd_a))
    asset = types.SimpleNamespace(body_names=body_names, cfg=types.SimpleNamespace(prim_path="/World/timing/env_.*/Robot"))
    start = time.perf_counter()
    big = mdp.robot_nominal_pose._read(stub_env(asset, many, "/World/timing/env_.*"), asset, asset_cfg)
    read_s = time.perf_counter() - start
    print(f" stage read over {len(many)} environments referencing A's file: {read_s:.3f} s, {big.shape}, "
          f"worst against pose_frames(B) {(big.cpu() - want_b).abs().max().item():.2e} rad")

    results.append((name, worst_frames, err_a, err_b, max(diffs.values()), min(moved.values()), obs_err_a, obs_err_b, kin_err, read_s))
    sim.clear_all_callbacks()
    sim.clear_instance()

print("\nSUMMARY family, localRot0 rule, A spawned (m), B in place (m), in place vs converted, smallest A to B change, "
      "observation A, observation B, observation vs realised, stage read of 1024 (s)")
for r in results:
    print("SUMMARY", tuple(round(v, 9) if isinstance(v, float) else v for v in r))
app.close()
```

## Appendix B, `co_optimisation/tests/sim_reward_invariance_check.py`

A new file, run the same way. Section 9.5 records its output.

```python
"""Invariance check of the nominal pose reward terms, run with Isaac Sim, not collected by pytest.

Each v2 is driven with a fake environment three ways, the attribute absent, the attribute
equal to the donor's height in every environment, and a per environment height, and is
compared with its original. The first two must equal the original exactly, the third
must equal the original evaluated environment by environment at that environment's
scaled parameter.
"""
import types

from isaaclab.app import AppLauncher

app = AppLauncher(headless=True).app

import torch  # noqa: E402

import environments.tasks.locomotion.mdp as mdp  # noqa: E402

print("mdp from", mdp.__file__)
torch.manual_seed(0)
N, F, R = 6, 4, 50
dev = "cpu"


class Obj(types.SimpleNamespace):
    pass


def make_env():
    root = torch.rand(N, 3) + torch.tensor([0, 0, 0.2])
    hits = torch.rand(N, R, 3) * 0.05
    hits[0, 3, 2] = float("inf")
    hits[2, 7, 2] = float("nan")
    xy = (torch.rand(N, R, 2) - 0.5) * 1.0
    hits[..., :2] = xy + root[:, None, :2]
    robot = Obj(data=Obj(root_pos_w=root,
                         body_pos_w=torch.cat([(torch.rand(N, F, 2) - 0.5) * 0.8 + root[:, None, :2], torch.rand(N, F, 1) * 0.15], -1),
                         body_lin_vel_w=torch.randn(N, F, 3),
                         body_quat_w=torch.tensor([1.0, 0, 0, 0]).repeat(N, F, 1)))
    scanner = Obj(data=Obj(ray_hits_w=hits))
    first = (torch.rand(N, F) > 0.5).float()
    air = torch.rand(N, F) * 0.8
    forces = torch.rand(N, 3, F, 3) * 3.0
    contact = Obj(data=Obj(last_air_time=air, net_forces_w_history=forces),
                  compute_first_contact=lambda dt: first.bool())
    sensors = {"height_scanner": scanner, "contact_forces": contact}

    class Scene(dict):
        pass
    scene = Scene(robot=robot)
    scene.sensors = sensors
    cmd = torch.rand(N, 3) * 2 - 1
    env = Obj(scene=scene, step_dt=0.02, num_envs=N, device=dev,
              command_manager=Obj(get_command=lambda name: cmd))
    return env


class Cfg:
    def __init__(self, name, ids=slice(None)):
        self.name, self.body_ids = name, ids


env = make_env()
robot_cfg = Cfg("robot")
scan_cfg = Cfg("height_scanner")
cs_cfg = Cfg("contact_forces")
fails = []


def check(name, a, b, tol=0.0):
    err = (a - b).abs().max().item()
    ok = err <= tol
    print(f"{'ok  ' if ok else 'FAIL'} {name:60s} max |diff| {err:.3e}")
    if not ok:
        fails.append(name)


heights = torch.tensor([0.20, 0.25, 0.292, 0.33, 0.37, 0.40])

ref = mdp.base_height_rough_l2(env, 0.292, scan_cfg, robot_cfg)
check("base_height v2, attribute absent", mdp.base_height_rough_l2_v2(env, 0.292, scan_cfg, robot_cfg), ref)
env.copt_nominal_base_height = torch.full((N,), 0.292)
check("base_height v2, donor height everywhere", mdp.base_height_rough_l2_v2(env, 0.292, scan_cfg, robot_cfg), ref, 1e-7)
env.copt_nominal_base_height = heights
per = torch.stack([mdp.base_height_rough_l2(env, float(h), scan_cfg, robot_cfg)[i] for i, h in enumerate(heights)])
check("base_height v2, per environment", mdp.base_height_rough_l2_v2(env, 0.292, scan_cfg, robot_cfg), per, 1e-7)
del env.copt_nominal_base_height

fr_cfg = Cfg("robot")
ref = mdp.feet_regulation(env, fr_cfg, 0.05, 0.804)
check("feet_regulation v2, attribute absent", mdp.feet_regulation_v2(env, fr_cfg, 0.05, 0.804), ref)
env.copt_nominal_base_height = torch.full((N,), 0.804)
check("feet_regulation v2, donor height everywhere", mdp.feet_regulation_v2(env, fr_cfg, 0.05, 0.804), ref, 1e-6)
env.copt_nominal_base_height = heights * 2.75
per = torch.stack([mdp.feet_regulation(env, fr_cfg, 0.05, float(h))[i] for i, h in enumerate(heights * 2.75)])
check("feet_regulation v2, per environment", mdp.feet_regulation_v2(env, fr_cfg, 0.05, 0.804), per, 1e-6)
del env.copt_nominal_base_height

for orig, new, ref_h, tmin, tmax in ((mdp.feet_air_time_v2, mdp.feet_air_time_v3, 0.292, 0.15, 0.45),
                                     (mdp.feet_air_time, mdp.feet_air_time_nominal, 0.804, 0.2, 0.5)):
    ref = orig(env, "base_velocity", cs_cfg, tmin, tmax)
    check(f"{new.__name__}, attribute absent", new(env, "base_velocity", cs_cfg, tmin, tmax, ref_h), ref)
    env.copt_nominal_base_height = torch.full((N,), ref_h)
    check(f"{new.__name__}, donor height everywhere", new(env, "base_velocity", cs_cfg, tmin, tmax, ref_h), ref, 1e-6)
    hh = heights * ref_h / 0.292
    env.copt_nominal_base_height = hh
    k = torch.sqrt(hh / ref_h)
    per = torch.stack([orig(env, "base_velocity", cs_cfg, tmin * float(k[i]), tmax * float(k[i]))[i] for i in range(N)])
    check(f"{new.__name__}, per environment", new(env, "base_velocity", cs_cfg, tmin, tmax, ref_h), per, 1e-6)
    del env.copt_nominal_base_height

args = dict(asset_cfg=Cfg("robot"), height_sensor_cfg=scan_cfg, target_height=0.10, std=0.05, tanh_mult=2.0, sensor_cfg=cs_cfg)
ref = mdp.foot_clearance_reward_v4(env, **args)
check("foot_clearance v6, attribute absent", mdp.foot_clearance_reward_v6(env, **args, target_height_frac=0.10 / 0.292), ref)
env.copt_nominal_base_height = torch.full((N,), 0.292)
check("foot_clearance v6, donor height everywhere", mdp.foot_clearance_reward_v6(env, **args, target_height_frac=0.10 / 0.292), ref, 1e-6)
check("foot_clearance v6, fraction unset", mdp.foot_clearance_reward_v6(env, **args), ref)
env.copt_nominal_base_height = heights
per = torch.stack([mdp.foot_clearance_reward_v4(env, **{**args, "target_height": float(h) * 0.10 / 0.292})[i] for i, h in enumerate(heights)])
check("foot_clearance v6, per environment", mdp.foot_clearance_reward_v6(env, **args, target_height_frac=0.10 / 0.292), per, 1e-6)
del env.copt_nominal_base_height

print("RESULT", "PASS" if not fails else f"FAIL {fails}")
app.close()
```

## Appendix C, `co_optimisation/tests/sim_asset_equivalence_check.py`

A new file, run the same way, once per robot against the committed document as it stood before phase 3, kept by section 4.4's step 4. Section 9.2 records its output.

```python
"""Physical equivalence of a committed asset before and after its fold moved into the body frames.

Run with Isaac Sim, not collected by pytest. Both documents are converted as usd_generator
converts a design and spawned apart in free fall. At random joint configurations, the old
one at q and the new one at q plus nothing but the fold it no longer carries, every body's
position relative to the root, its centre of mass, its world inertia tensor and its mass
are compared, and the joint limits after the same shift. Both are then driven by the same
targets through 200 physics steps and their trajectories compared. The bodies whose inertia
differs in their own frame, which the robot_inertia observation reads, are listed.

    ./isaaclab.sh -p biped/co_optimisation/tests/sim_asset_equivalence_check.py FAMILY OLD.xml NEW.xml
"""
import os
import sys
import tempfile

from isaaclab.app import AppLauncher

app = AppLauncher(headless=True).app

import torch  # noqa: E402

import isaaclab.sim as sim_utils  # noqa: E402
from isaaclab.actuators import ImplicitActuatorCfg  # noqa: E402
from isaaclab.assets import Articulation, ArticulationCfg  # noqa: E402
from isaaclab.sim.converters import MjcfConverter, MjcfConverterCfg  # noqa: E402
from isaaclab.utils.math import matrix_from_quat  # noqa: E402

from design_generator import biped, quadruped  # noqa: E402

family, old_xml, new_xml = sys.argv[1:4]
builder = {"biped": biped, "quadruped": quadruped}[family].builder
root_prim = {"quadruped": "/trunk/trunk", "biped": "/base_Link/base_Link"}[family]
fold = ({"thigh_joint": builder.THIGH_BEND_RAD, "calf_joint": builder.SHANK_BEND_RAD}
        if family == "quadruped" else {})
out = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="copt_equiv_")

sim = sim_utils.SimulationContext(sim_utils.SimulationCfg(dt=0.005, device="cuda:0"))
robots = {}
for i, (tag, xml) in enumerate((("old", old_xml), ("new", new_xml))):
    usd = MjcfConverter(MjcfConverterCfg(
        asset_path=xml, usd_dir=os.path.join(out, tag), usd_file_name=f"{tag}.usd", link_density=0.0,
        import_inertia_tensor=True, fix_base=False, self_collision=False, make_instanceable=True,
        force_usd_conversion=True)).usd_path
    sim_utils.UsdFileCfg(usd_path=usd).func(f"/World/{tag}/Robot", sim_utils.UsdFileCfg(usd_path=usd))
    nominal = {".*": 0.0}
    if tag == "old" and fold:
        nominal = {".*_hip_joint": 0.0, **{f".*_{k}": v for k, v in fold.items()}}
    robots[tag] = Articulation(ArticulationCfg(
        prim_path=f"/World/{tag}/Robot", spawn=None, articulation_root_prim_path=root_prim,
        init_state=ArticulationCfg.InitialStateCfg(joint_pos=nominal),
        actuators={"all": ImplicitActuatorCfg(joint_names_expr=[".*"], stiffness=40.0, damping=1.0)}))
sim.reset()
old, new = robots["old"], robots["new"]
assert old.joint_names == new.joint_names and old.body_names == new.body_names
shift = torch.tensor([[next((v for k, v in fold.items() if n.endswith(k)), 0.0) for n in new.joint_names]],
                     device=new.device)


def place(robot, q, x):
    pose = torch.tensor([[x, 0.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0]], device=robot.device)
    robot.write_root_pose_to_sim(pose)
    robot.write_root_velocity_to_sim(torch.zeros(1, 6, device=robot.device))
    robot.write_joint_state_to_sim(q, torch.zeros_like(q))


def relative(robot):
    d = robot.data
    rot = matrix_from_quat(d.body_link_quat_w[0])
    inertia = rot @ d.default_inertia[0].to(rot.device).view(-1, 3, 3) @ rot.transpose(1, 2)
    return (d.body_link_pos_w[0] - d.root_pos_w[0], d.body_com_pos_w[0] - d.root_pos_w[0], inertia)


worst = {"body": 0.0, "com": 0.0, "inertia": 0.0}
lo, hi = new.data.joint_pos_limits[0, :, 0], new.data.joint_pos_limits[0, :, 1]
limits = (old.data.joint_pos_limits[0] - (new.data.joint_pos_limits[0] + shift[0, :, None])).abs().max().item()
mass = (old.data.default_mass - new.data.default_mass).abs().max().item()
torch.manual_seed(0)
for _ in range(20):
    q_new = (lo + (hi - lo) * (0.1 + 0.8 * torch.rand_like(lo))).unsqueeze(0)
    place(old, q_new + shift, 0.0)
    place(new, q_new, 0.0)
    for key, a, b in zip(worst, relative(old), relative(new)):
        worst[key] = max(worst[key], (a - b).abs().max().item())
body_frame = (old.data.default_inertia[0] - new.data.default_inertia[0]).abs().amax(dim=-1)
changed = [n for n, d in zip(new.body_names, body_frame.tolist()) if d > 1e-7]
print(f"[{family}] inertia in the body frame, robot_inertia's channels, differs for {changed}")
print(f"[{family}] static, 20 configurations: body {worst['body']:.2e} m, com {worst['com']:.2e} m, "
      f"world inertia {worst['inertia']:.2e} kg m^2, mass {mass:.2e} kg, joint limits {limits:.2e} rad")

q_new = (lo + (hi - lo) * 0.5).unsqueeze(0)
target = (lo + (hi - lo) * (0.3 + 0.4 * torch.rand_like(lo))).unsqueeze(0)
place(old, q_new + shift, 0.0)
place(new, q_new, 5.0)
old.set_joint_position_target(target + shift)
new.set_joint_position_target(target)
start = {t: r.data.root_pos_w[0].clone() for t, r in robots.items()}
drift = 0.0
for _ in range(200):
    for r in robots.values():
        r.write_data_to_sim()
    sim.step(render=False)
    for r in robots.values():
        r.update(sim.get_physics_dt())
    a, b = relative(old)[0], relative(new)[0]
    fall = (old.data.root_pos_w[0] - start["old"]) - (new.data.root_pos_w[0] - start["new"])
    drift = max(drift, (a - b).abs().max().item(), fall.abs().max().item())
joint = (old.data.joint_pos - shift - new.data.joint_pos).abs().max().item()
print(f"[{family}] dynamic, 200 steps under the same targets: body and root {drift:.2e} m, joints {joint:.2e} rad")
app.close()
```
